            if okFilter and allowed then
                local character = player.Character
                local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
                    for _, part in candidateParts(character) do
                        local screen, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                        if onScreen and screen.Z > 0 then
                            local distance = screenDistance(screen.X, screen.Y, centerX, centerY)
                            if distance <= self.Config.FOV and distance < bestDistance then
                                bestPart, bestDistance = part, distance
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        return bestPart
    end

    local function onRenderStep(dt)
        if not enabled then return end

        if self.Config.Mode == "Button" then
            if not held then
                status = "waiting for the AIM button"
                return
            end
        elseif self.Config.Mode ~= "Always" and not isKeyHeld(self.Config.Key) then
            status = "waiting for the aim key"
            return
        end
        if self.Config.OnlyWithTool and not holdsTool() then
            status = "no tool in hand"
            return
        end
        if self.Config.PauseOnMouseMove and not touchOnly then
            local delta = UserInputService:GetMouseDelta()
            if delta.Magnitude > MOUSE_PAUSE_THRESHOLD then
                status = "paused (you are moving the mouse)"
                return
            end
        end

        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then
            status = "no camera"
            return
        end

        local part = findTarget(camera)
        if not part then
            status = "no target inside the FOV circle"
            return
        end

        status = "locked on " .. tostring(part.Parent and part.Parent.Name or "?")
        local aimPoint = predictPoint(part.Position, part.AssemblyLinearVelocity, self.Config.Prediction)
        local goal = CFrame.lookAt(camera.CFrame.Position, aimPoint)
        local alpha = shapeAlpha(self.Config.SmoothMode, baseAlpha(self.Config.Smooth, dt))
        camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame:Lerp(goal, alpha)
    end

    function self.SetEnabled(state)
        enabled = state and true or false

        if enabled and not bound then
            RunService:BindToRenderStep(BIND_NAME, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, onRenderStep)
            bound = true
            status = "on"
        elseif not enabled and bound then
            RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(BIND_NAME)
            bound = false
            status = "off"
        end
    end

    -- A short sentence about why the aim is or is not moving the camera right now.
    function self.GetStatus()
        return status
    end

    -- Stops aiming. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        self.SetEnabled(false)
    end

    -- Exposed for isolated checks.
    self._internal = {
        screenDistance = screenDistance,
        baseAlpha = baseAlpha,
        shapeAlpha = shapeAlpha,
        predictPoint = predictPoint,
    }

    return self
end

return Aim
end)()
local AimController = Aim.new({
    Players = game:GetService("Players"),
    RunService = RunService,
    UserInputService = UserInputService,
    Workspace = Workspace,
})
Backshot.Aim = AimController
--
--
-- ESP module (backshot_esp.lua)
local ESP = (function()
-- backshot_esp.lua
-- Player ESP written from scratch on public Roblox APIs.
--
-- Usage:
--   local ESP = loadstring(readfile("backshot/backshot_esp.lua"))()
--   local esp = ESP.new({
--       Players = game:GetService("Players"),
--       RunService = game:GetService("RunService"),
--       Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace"),
--   })
--   esp.Config.Color = function(player) return Color3.new(1, 0, 0) end
--   esp.SetEnabled(true)
--   esp.Unload()
--
-- Visuals:
--   * Highlight outline on the whole character (depth-tested off, visible through walls).
--   * Billboard above the head: name, health, distance in studs.
--
-- Game rules (roles, teams, who counts as an enemy) go in Config.Filter and Config.Color.
-- Nothing here is MM2-specific.

local ESP = {}

local function formatHealth(humanoid)
    return string.format("%d/%d", math.floor(humanoid.Health), math.floor(humanoid.MaxHealth))
end

function ESP.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local RunService = env.RunService
    local Workspace = env.Workspace

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local enabled = false
    local connections = {}
    local entries = {} -- [Player] = { highlight, billboard, label, character }

    self.Config = {
        MaxDistance = 1000,       -- studs; beyond this the visuals are hidden
        ShowHighlight = true,
        ShowName = true,
        ShowHealth = true,
        ShowDistance = true,
        ExtraText = nil, -- optional function(player) -> string appended to the label
        FillTransparency = 0.7,
        OutlineTransparency = 0,
        -- Return true if this player should get ESP. Default: everyone except you.
        Filter = function(player)
            return player ~= LocalPlayer
        end,
        -- Colour for this player's outline and text. Default: white.
        Color = function(player)
            return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        end,
    }

    local function track(connection)
        table.insert(connections, connection)
        return connection
    end

    local function destroyEntry(player)
        local entry = entries[player]
        if not entry then return end
        if entry.highlight then entry.highlight:Destroy() end
        if entry.billboard then entry.billboard:Destroy() end
        entries[player] = nil
    end

    local function buildEntry(player, character)
        destroyEntry(player)

        local okFilter, allowed = pcall(self.Config.Filter, player)
        if not okFilter or not allowed then return end

        local highlight, billboard, label
        if self.Config.ShowHighlight then
            highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
            highlight.Adornee = character
            highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            highlight.Parent = character
        end

        local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then
            billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            billboard.Adornee = head
            billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 44)
            billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
            billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
            billboard.Parent = character

            label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            label.TextSize = 14
            label.TextWrapped = true
            label.Parent = billboard
        end

        entries[player] = {
            highlight = highlight,
            billboard = billboard,
            label = label,
            character = character,
        }
    end

    local function refreshEntry(player, entry)
        local character = entry.character
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        -- The character was replaced or died: drop the entry, the next CharacterAdded rebuilds it.
        if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not root then
            destroyEntry(player)
            return
        end

        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local distance = camera and (camera.CFrame.Position - root.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
        local visible = distance <= self.Config.MaxDistance

        local okColor, color = pcall(self.Config.Color, player)
        if not okColor or typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then
            color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        end
        if entry.highlight then
            entry.highlight.Enabled = visible
            entry.highlight.FillColor = color
            entry.highlight.OutlineColor = color
            entry.highlight.FillTransparency = self.Config.FillTransparency
            entry.highlight.OutlineTransparency = self.Config.OutlineTransparency
        end
        if entry.billboard then
            entry.billboard.Enabled = visible
        end
        if entry.label and visible then
            local parts = {}
            if self.Config.ShowName then table.insert(parts, player.Name) end
            if self.Config.ExtraText then
                local okExtra, extra = pcall(self.Config.ExtraText, player)
                if okExtra and type(extra) == "string" and extra ~= "" then table.insert(parts, extra) end
            end
            if self.Config.ShowHealth then table.insert(parts, formatHealth(humanoid)) end
            if self.Config.ShowDistance then table.insert(parts, string.format("%d st", math.floor(distance))) end
            entry.label.Text = table.concat(parts, "  ")
            entry.label.TextColor3 = color
        end
    end

    local function onPlayerAdded(player)
        track(player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
            if enabled then buildEntry(player, character) end
        end))
        track(player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
            destroyEntry(player)
        end))
        if enabled and player.Character then
            buildEntry(player, player.Character)
        end
    end

    local renderConnection

    function self.SetEnabled(state)
        enabled = state and true or false

        if not enabled then
            for player in pairs(entries) do
                destroyEntry(player)
            end
            if renderConnection and renderConnection.Connected then
                renderConnection:Disconnect()
            end
            renderConnection = nil
            return
        end

        for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
            if player.Character then
                buildEntry(player, player.Character)
            end
        end
        if not renderConnection then
            renderConnection = track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                for player, entry in pairs(entries) do
                    refreshEntry(player, entry)
                end
            end))
        end
    end

    -- Rebuild after changing options that affect what is created (highlight/billboard on or off, filter).
    function self.Rebuild()
        if not enabled then return end
        for player in pairs(entries) do
            destroyEntry(player)
        end
        for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
            if player.Character then
                buildEntry(player, player.Character)
            end
        end
    end

    -- Disconnects everything and removes every visual this module created.
    function self.Unload()
        enabled = false
        for player in pairs(entries) do
            destroyEntry(player)
        end
        for _, connection in connections do
            if connection.Connected then
                connection:Disconnect()
            end
        end
        table.clear(connections)
        renderConnection = nil
    end

    track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(onPlayerAdded))
    track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
        destroyEntry(player)
    end))
    for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
        onPlayerAdded(player)
    end

    return self
end

return ESP

end)()
local ESPController = ESP.new({
    Players = game:GetService("Players"),
    RunService = RunService,
    Workspace = Workspace,
})
Backshot.ESP = ESPController
--
--
-- Animation packs for every slot (backshot_anim.lua)
local AnimModule = (function()
local Anim = {}

local PACKS = {
    Zombie = {idle1 = 616158929, idle2 = 616160636, walk = 616168032, run = 616163682, jump = 616161997, climb = 616156119, fall = 616157476, swim = 616165109, swimidle = 616166655},
    Ninja = {idle1 = 656117400, idle2 = 656118341, walk = 656121766, run = 656118852, jump = 656117878, climb = 656114359, fall = 656115606, swim = 656119721, swimidle = 656121397},
    Robot = {idle1 = 616088211, idle2 = 616089559, walk = 616095330, run = 616091570, jump = 616090535, climb = 616086039, fall = 616087089, swim = 616092998, swimidle = 616094091},
    Stylish = {idle1 = 616136790, idle2 = 616138447, walk = 616146177, run = 616140816, jump = 616139451, climb = 616133594, fall = 616134815, swim = 616143378, swimidle = 616144772},
    Cartoony = {idle1 = 742637544, idle2 = 742638445, walk = 742640026, run = 742638842, jump = 742637942, climb = 742636889, fall = 742637151, swim = 742639220, swimidle = 742639812},
    Superhero = {idle1 = 616111295, idle2 = 616113536, walk = 616122287, run = 616117076, jump = 616115533, climb = 616104706, fall = 616108001, swim = 616119360, swimidle = 616120861},
    Knight = {idle1 = 657595757, idle2 = 657568135, walk = 657552124, run = 657564596, jump = 658409194, climb = 658360781, fall = 657600338, swim = 657560551, swimidle = 657557095},
    Levitation = {idle1 = 616006778, idle2 = 616008087, walk = 616013216, run = 616010382, jump = 616008936, climb = 616003713, fall = 616005863, swim = 616011509, swimidle = 616012453},
    Astronaut = {idle1 = 891621366, idle2 = 891633237, walk = 891667138, run = 891636393, jump = 891627522, climb = 891609353, fall = 891617961, swim = 891639666, swimidle = 891663592},
    Vampire = {idle1 = 1083445855, idle2 = 1083450166, walk = 1083473930, run = 1083462077, jump = 1083455352, climb = 1083439238, fall = 1083443587, swim = 1083222527, swimidle = 1083225406},
    Werewolf = {idle1 = 1083195517, idle2 = 1083214717, walk = 1083178339, run = 1083216690, jump = 1083218792, climb = 1083182000, fall = 1083189019, swim = 1083222527, swimidle = 1083225406},
    Toy = {idle1 = 782841498, idle2 = 782845736, walk = 782843345, run = 782842708, jump = 782847020, climb = 782843869, fall = 782846423, swim = 782844582, swimidle = 782845186},
    Pirate = {idle1 = 750781874, idle2 = 750782770, walk = 750785693, run = 750783738, jump = 750782230, climb = 750779899, fall = 750780242, swim = 750784579, swimidle = 750785176},
    Elder = {idle1 = 845397899, idle2 = 845400520, walk = 845403856, run = 845386501, jump = 845398858, climb = 845392038, fall = 845396048, swim = 845401742, swimidle = 845403127},
    Mage = {idle1 = 707742142, idle2 = 707855907, walk = 707897309, run = 707861613, jump = 707853694, climb = 707826056, fall = 707829716, swim = 707876443, swimidle = 707894699},
    Bubbly = {idle1 = 910004836, idle2 = 910009958, walk = 910034870, run = 910025107, jump = 910016857, fall = 910001910, swim = 910028158, swimidle = 910030921},
    Rthro = {idle1 = 2510196951, idle2 = 2510197257, walk = 2510202577, run = 2510198475, jump = 2510197830, climb = 2510192778, fall = 2510195892, swim = 2510199791, swimidle = 2510201162},
    Oldschool = {idle1 = 5319828216, idle2 = 5319831086, walk = 5319847204, run = 5319844329, jump = 5319841935, climb = 5319816685, fall = 5319839762, swim = 5319850266, swimidle = 5319852613},
}

Anim.PackNames = { "Default" }
do
    local sorted = {}
    for name in pairs(PACKS) do
        table.insert(sorted, name)
    end
    table.sort(sorted)
    for _, name in sorted do
        table.insert(Anim.PackNames, name)
    end
end

Anim.SlotNames = { "Idle", "Walk", "Run", "Jump", "Fall", "Climb", "Swim" }

-- slot -> folder inside Animate, and which Animation object takes which pack key.
local SLOTS = {
    { slot = "Idle", folder = "idle", objects = { Animation1 = "idle1", Animation2 = "idle2" } },
    { slot = "Walk", folder = "walk", objects = { WalkAnim = "walk" } },
    { slot = "Run", folder = "run", objects = { RunAnim = "run" } },
    { slot = "Jump", folder = "jump", objects = { JumpAnim = "jump" } },
    { slot = "Fall", folder = "fall", objects = { FallAnim = "fall" } },
    { slot = "Climb", folder = "climb", objects = { ClimbAnim = "climb" } },
    { slot = "Swim", folder = "swim", objects = { Swim = "swim" } },
    { slot = "Swim", folder = "swimidle", objects = { SwimIdle = "swimidle" } },
}

-- Pure helper: the animation id a slot should use, or nil to keep the original.
-- `packName` "Default" (or unknown) and a pack missing that key both give nil.
local function chooseId(packs, packName, key)
    local pack = packs[packName]
    return pack and pack[key] or nil
end

function Anim.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local originals = {}
    local connection
    local enabled = false

    self.Choice = {}
    for _, slot in Anim.SlotNames do
        self.Choice[slot] = "Default"
    end

    local function apply()
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local animate = character and character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if not animate then return end

        for _, definition in SLOTS do
            local folder = animate:FindFirstChild(definition.folder)
            if folder then
                for objectName, key in pairs(definition.objects) do
                    local object = folder:FindFirstChild(objectName)
                    if object and object:IsA("Animation") then
                        if originals[object] == nil then
                            originals[object] = object.AnimationId
                        end
                        local id = chooseId(PACKS, self.Choice[definition.slot], key)
                        if enabled and id then
                            object.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
                        else
                            object.AnimationId = originals[object]
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function restore()
        for object, original in pairs(originals) do
            if object.Parent then
                object.AnimationId = original
            end
        end
        table.clear(originals)
    end

    function self.Apply()
        apply()
    end

    function self.SetPack(slot, packName)
        if self.Choice[slot] == nil then return end
        self.Choice[slot] = packName
        apply()
    end

    function self.SetAll(packName)
        for _, slot in Anim.SlotNames do
            self.Choice[slot] = packName
        end
        apply()
    end

    function self.SetEnabled(state)
        enabled = state and true or false

        if enabled and not connection then
            -- A respawn builds a new Animate script, so forget the old objects and apply again.
            connection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                task.wait(1)
                table.clear(originals)
                apply()
            end)
        elseif not enabled and connection then
            connection:Disconnect()
            connection = nil
        end

        apply()
        if not enabled then
            restore()
        end
    end

    -- Puts every original animation id back and disconnects. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        enabled = false
        if connection then
            connection:Disconnect()
            connection = nil
        end
        restore()
    end

    -- Exposed for isolated checks.
    self._internal = { chooseId = chooseId }

    return self
end

return Anim
end)()
local AnimController = AnimModule.new({ Players = Players })
Backshot.Anim = AnimController
--
--
-- Box and tracer ESP (backshot_boxes.lua)
local Boxes = (function()
local Boxes = {}

local function drawingAvailable()
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local probe = Drawing.new("Line")
        probe:Remove()
    end)
    return ok
end

function Boxes.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local RunService = env.RunService
    local Workspace = env.Workspace

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local enabled = false
    local connections = {}
    local entries = {} -- [Player] = { box = Drawing, tracer = Drawing }

    self.Config = {
        Box = true,
        Tracers = true,
        Skeleton = false,
        Thickness = 1,
        Transparency = 1,
        MaxDistance = 1000,
        Filter = function(player)
            return player ~= LocalPlayer
        end,
        Color = function(player)
            return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        end,
    }

    -- Joint pairs for skeleton lines. R15 segments are tried first; whichever parts the
    -- character actually has (R15 or R6) decide which lines end up drawn.
    local SKELETON_SEGMENTS = {
        { "Head", "UpperTorso" }, { "Head", "Torso" },
        { "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso" },
        { "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg" }, { "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg" }, { "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot" },
        { "LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg" }, { "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg" }, { "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot" },
        { "UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm" }, { "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm" }, { "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand" },
        { "UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm" }, { "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm" }, { "RightLowerArm", "RightHand" },
        { "Torso", "Left Arm" }, { "Torso", "Right Arm" }, { "Torso", "Left Leg" }, { "Torso", "Right Leg" },
    }

    local function track(connection)
        table.insert(connections, connection)
        return connection
    end

    local function removeEntry(player)
        local entry = entries[player]
        if not entry then return end
        if entry.box then entry.box:Remove() end
        if entry.tracer then entry.tracer:Remove() end
        for _, line in entry.skeletonLines do
            line:Remove()
        end
        entries[player] = nil
    end

    local function ensureEntry(player)
        local entry = entries[player]
        if entry then return entry end
        entry = {
            box = Drawing.new("Square"),
            tracer = Drawing.new("Line"),
            skeletonLines = {},
        }
        entry.box.Filled = false
        entry.box.Visible = false
        entry.tracer.Visible = false
        entries[player] = entry
        return entry
    end

    local function ensureSkeletonLine(entry, index)
        local line = entry.skeletonLines[index]
        if not line then
            line = Drawing.new("Line")
            line.Visible = false
            entry.skeletonLines[index] = line
        end
        return line
    end

    local function drawSkeleton(entry, camera, character)
        local used = 0
        for _, segment in SKELETON_SEGMENTS do
            local partA = character:FindFirstChild(segment[1])
            local partB = character:FindFirstChild(segment[2])
            if partA and partB then
                local screenA, onA = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(partA.Position)
                local screenB, onB = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(partB.Position)
                if onA and onB and screenA.Z > 0 and screenB.Z > 0 then
                    used += 1
                    local line = ensureSkeletonLine(entry, used)
                    line.From = Vector2.new(screenA.X, screenA.Y)
                    line.To = Vector2.new(screenB.X, screenB.Y)
                    line.Color = self.Config.Color(entry._player or LocalPlayer)
                    line.Thickness = self.Config.Thickness
                    line.Visible = true
                end
            end
        end
        for index = used + 1, #entry.skeletonLines do
            entry.skeletonLines[index].Visible = false
        end
    end

    -- Pure helper: screen rectangle from projected top and bottom points.
    local function boxFromPoints(topX, topY, botY)
        local height = math.abs(botY - topY)
        local width = height * 0.5
        return topX - width / 2, topY, width, height
    end

    local function hideEntry(entry)
        if entry.box then entry.box.Visible = false end
        if entry.tracer then entry.tracer.Visible = false end
        for _, line in entry.skeletonLines do
            line.Visible = false
        end
    end

    local function render()
        if not enabled then return end
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end
        local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
        local centerBottom = Vector2.new(viewport.X / 2, viewport.Y)

        for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
            local okFilter, allowed = pcall(self.Config.Filter, player)
            local character = player.Character
            local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

            local valid = okFilter and allowed and root and head and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0
            if valid then
                local distance = (camera.CFrame.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                valid = distance <= self.Config.MaxDistance
            end

            if not valid then
                local entry = entries[player]
                if entry then hideEntry(entry) end
            else
                local top, topOn = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.9, 0))
                local bottom, bottomOn = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
                if top.Z <= 0 or bottom.Z <= 0 then
                    local entry = entries[player]
                    if entry then hideEntry(entry) end
                else
                    local entry = ensureEntry(player)
                    entry._player = player
                    local color = self.Config.Color(player)
                    local x, y, w, h = boxFromPoints(top.X, top.Y, bottom.Y)

                    entry.box.Visible = self.Config.Box and true or false
                    entry.box.Position = Vector2.new(x, y)
                    entry.box.Size = Vector2.new(w, h)
                    entry.box.Color = color
                    entry.box.Thickness = self.Config.Thickness
                    entry.box.Transparency = self.Config.Transparency

                    entry.tracer.Visible = self.Config.Tracers and true or false
                    entry.tracer.From = centerBottom
                    entry.tracer.To = Vector2.new(bottom.X, bottom.Y)
                    entry.tracer.Color = color
                    entry.tracer.Thickness = self.Config.Thickness
                    entry.tracer.Transparency = self.Config.Transparency

                    if self.Config.Skeleton then
                        drawSkeleton(entry, camera, character)
                    else
                        for _, line in entry.skeletonLines do
                            line.Visible = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    function self.SetEnabled(state)
        if state and not drawingAvailable() then
            enabled = false
            return false
        end

        enabled = state and true or false

        if not enabled then
            for player in pairs(entries) do
                removeEntry(player)
            end
            return true
        end

        if not connections.render then
            connections.render = track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(render))
            connections.leave = track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(removeEntry))
        end
        return true
    end

    -- Removes every drawing and disconnects everything. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        enabled = false
        for player in pairs(entries) do
            removeEntry(player)
        end
        for _, connection in connections do
            if typeof(connection) == "RBXScriptConnection" and connection.Connected then
                connection:Disconnect()
            end
        end
        table.clear(connections)
    end

    -- Exposed for isolated checks.
    self._internal = { boxFromPoints = boxFromPoints }

    return self
end

return Boxes
end)()
local BoxController = Boxes.new({
    Players = game:GetService("Players"),
    RunService = RunService,
    Workspace = Workspace,
})
Backshot.Boxes = BoxController
--
--
-- Ghost mode (backshot_ghost.lua)
local Ghost = (function()
local Ghost = {}

-- Pure helper: clamps each axis of `position` to within `radius` studs of `center`.
-- Kept free of Roblox objects so it can be checked on its own.
local function clampToBox(position, center, radius)
    local dx = math.clamp(position.X - center.X, -radius, radius)
    local dy = math.clamp(position.Y - center.Y, -radius, radius)
    local dz = math.clamp(position.Z - center.Z, -radius, radius)
    return Vector3.new(center.X + dx, center.Y + dy, center.Z + dz)
end

function Ghost.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local RunService = env.RunService
    local Workspace = env.Workspace

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local active = false
    local connections = {}

    local savedCFrame, savedSubject
    local realRoot, realHumanoid
    local decoy

    self.Config = {
        HidePosition = Vector3.new(0, 10000, 0),
        -- How far you can run from HidePosition before being pulled back inside the box.
        SafeRadius = 150,
    }

    local function track(connection)
        table.insert(connections, connection)
        return connection
    end

    local function buildDecoy(character)
        local wasArchivable = character.Archivable
        character.Archivable = true
        local clone = character:Clone()
        character.Archivable = wasArchivable
        if not clone then return nil end

        for _, descendant in clone:GetDescendants() do
            if descendant:IsA("BaseScript") then
                descendant:Destroy()
            elseif descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                descendant.Anchored = true
                descendant.CanCollide = false
            end
        end

        local cloneHumanoid = clone:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if cloneHumanoid then
            cloneHumanoid.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
        end

        clone.Name = "GhostDecoy"
        return clone, cloneHumanoid
    end

    function self.Start()
        if active then return true end

        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not (root and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and camera) then
            return false
        end

        local clone, cloneHumanoid = buildDecoy(character)
        if not clone or not cloneHumanoid then
            if clone then clone:Destroy() end
            return false
        end

        savedCFrame = root.CFrame
        savedSubject = camera.CameraSubject
        realRoot, realHumanoid = root, humanoid

        clone.Parent = Workspace
        clone:PivotTo(savedCFrame)
        decoy = clone
        camera.CameraSubject = cloneHumanoid

        -- One-time move into the hidden area. After this, the character is free to
        -- run and strafe there; we only step in if it drifts out of the safe box.
        realRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(self.Config.HidePosition)
        realRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

        active = true
        track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not active then return end
            if not (realRoot and realRoot.Parent) or not realHumanoid or realHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
                self.Stop()
                return
            end

            local position = realRoot.Position
            local clamped = clampToBox(position, self.Config.HidePosition, self.Config.SafeRadius)
            if (clamped - position).Magnitude > 0.5 then
                realRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(clamped) * (realRoot.CFrame - realRoot.CFrame.Position)
            end
        end))
        return true
    end

    function self.Stop()
        if not active then return end
        active = false

        for _, connection in connections do
            if connection.Connected then
                connection:Disconnect()
            end
        end
        table.clear(connections)

        if realRoot and realRoot.Parent and savedCFrame then
            realRoot.CFrame = savedCFrame
            realRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end

        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera then
            if realHumanoid and realHumanoid.Parent then
                camera.CameraSubject = realHumanoid
            elseif savedSubject then
                camera.CameraSubject = savedSubject
            end
        end

        if decoy then
            decoy:Destroy()
            decoy = nil
        end
    end

    -- Plain on/off, same shape as every other feature's SetEnabled/EnableX.
    function self.SetGhost(state)
        if state then
            return self.Start()
        else
            self.Stop()
            return true
        end
    end

    function self.IsActive()
        return active
    end

    -- Returns you to the saved position and removes everything this module made.
    function self.Unload()
        self.Stop()
        for _, connection in connections do
            if connection.Connected then
                connection:Disconnect()
            end
        end
        table.clear(connections)
    end

    -- Exposed for isolated checks.
    self._internal = { clampToBox = clampToBox }

    return self
end

return Ghost
end)()
local GhostController = Ghost.new({
    Players = game:GetService("Players"),
    RunService = RunService,
    Workspace = Workspace,
})
Backshot.Ghost = GhostController
--
--
-- World visuals (backshot_visuals.lua)
local WorldVisuals = (function()
local Visuals = {}

local LIGHTING_PROPS = {
    "Brightness", "Ambient", "OutdoorAmbient", "GlobalShadows",
    "FogStart", "FogEnd", "FogColor", "ClockTime", "GeographicLatitude",
}

-- Native Lighting/Sky properties only, no texture ids: this only tweaks colours, the sun
-- and moon size, star count, and fog, since I cannot fetch and verify a real skybox texture here.
local SKY_PRESETS = {
    Day = { ambient = Color3.fromRGB(150,150,150), outdoor = Color3.fromRGB(150,150,150), brightness = 2, clockTime = 14, fogColor = Color3.fromRGB(192,192,192), sunSize = 21, moonSize = 11, stars = 3000 },
    Sunset = { ambient = Color3.fromRGB(140,90,70), outdoor = Color3.fromRGB(180,110,70), brightness = 1.4, clockTime = 18.2, fogColor = Color3.fromRGB(200,120,80), sunSize = 28, moonSize = 11, stars = 3000 },
    Night = { ambient = Color3.fromRGB(20,20,35), outdoor = Color3.fromRGB(25,25,45), brightness = 0.3, clockTime = 1, fogColor = Color3.fromRGB(10,10,20), sunSize = 21, moonSize = 18, stars = 6000 },
    Alien = { ambient = Color3.fromRGB(60,120,60), outdoor = Color3.fromRGB(40,160,80), brightness = 1.6, clockTime = 21, fogColor = Color3.fromRGB(40,120,60), sunSize = 35, moonSize = 30, stars = 9000 },
}

local function drawingAvailable()
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local probe = Drawing.new("Line")
        probe:Remove()
    end)
    return ok
end

function Visuals.new(env)
    local RunService = env.RunService
    local Workspace = env.Workspace
    local Lighting = env.Lighting

    local self = {}
    local connections = {}
    local flags = { Fullbright = false, NoFog = false, Time = false }
    local saved = nil
    local savedAtmosphere = {}
    local circleEnabled, crosshairEnabled = false, false
    local circle, crossH, crossV

    self.Config = {
        ClockTime = 12,
        SkyPreset = "Day",
        CircleRadius = 120,
        CircleColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        CrosshairSize = 8,
        CrosshairGap = 3,
        CrosshairColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
    }

    local function disconnect(key)
        local connection = connections[key]
        if connection and connection.Connected then
            connection:Disconnect()
        end
        connections[key] = nil
    end

    -- Lighting -----------------------------------------------------------

    local function anyLighting()
        return flags.Fullbright or flags.NoFog or flags.Time
    end

    local function snapshot()
        if saved then return end
        saved = {}
        for _, property in LIGHTING_PROPS do
            saved[property] = Lighting[property]
        end
        savedAtmosphere = {}
        for _, child in Lighting:GetChildren() do
            if child:IsA("Atmosphere") then
                savedAtmosphere[child] = { Density = child.Density, Haze = child.Haze }
            end
        end
    end

    local function restore()
        if not saved then return end
        for property, value in pairs(saved) do
            pcall(function() Lighting[property] = value end)
        end
        for atmosphere, values in pairs(savedAtmosphere) do
            if atmosphere.Parent then
                atmosphere.Density = values.Density
                atmosphere.Haze = values.Haze
            end
        end
        saved = nil
        savedAtmosphere = {}
    end

    local function setIfDifferent(property, value)
        if Lighting[property] ~= value then
            Lighting[property] = value
        end
    end

    local function applyLighting()
        if flags.Fullbright then
            setIfDifferent("Brightness", 2)
            setIfDifferent("Ambient", Color3.fromRGB(178, 178, 178))
            setIfDifferent("OutdoorAmbient", Color3.fromRGB(178, 178, 178))
            setIfDifferent("GlobalShadows", false)
        end
        if flags.NoFog then
            setIfDifferent("FogStart", 1000000)
            setIfDifferent("FogEnd", 1000000)
            for _, child in Lighting:GetChildren() do
                if child:IsA("Atmosphere") then
                    if child.Density ~= 0 then child.Density = 0 end
                    if child.Haze ~= 0 then child.Haze = 0 end
                end
            end
        end
        if flags.Time then
            setIfDifferent("ClockTime", self.Config.ClockTime)
        end
    end

    -- Rebuilds from scratch so turning one option off does not leave another half-applied.
    local function refreshLighting()
        restore()
        if anyLighting() then
            snapshot()
            if not connections.lighting then
                connections.lighting = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(applyLighting)
            end
            applyLighting()
        else
            disconnect("lighting")
        end
    end

    function self.SetFullbright(state)
        flags.Fullbright = state and true or false
        refreshLighting()
    end

    function self.SetNoFog(state)
        flags.NoFog = state and true or false
        refreshLighting()
    end

    function self.SetTime(state)
        flags.Time = state and true or false
        refreshLighting()
    end

    function self.SetSky(name)
        local preset = SKY_PRESETS[name]
        if not preset then return end
        self.Config.SkyPreset = name
        self.Config.ClockTime = preset.clockTime
        flags.Time = true

        snapshot()
        setIfDifferent("Ambient", preset.ambient)
        setIfDifferent("OutdoorAmbient", preset.outdoor)
        setIfDifferent("Brightness", preset.brightness)
        setIfDifferent("FogColor", preset.fogColor)
        setIfDifferent("ClockTime", preset.clockTime)

        local sky = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
        if sky then
            sky.CelestialBodiesShown = true
            sky.SunAngularSize = preset.sunSize
            sky.MoonAngularSize = preset.moonSize
            sky.StarCount = preset.stars
        end

        if not connections.lighting then
            connections.lighting = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(applyLighting)
        end
    end

    -- Drawings -----------------------------------------------------------

    local function removeDrawings()
        if circle then circle:Remove() circle = nil end
        if crossH then crossH:Remove() crossH = nil end
        if crossV then crossV:Remove() crossV = nil end
    end

    local function renderDrawings()
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end
        local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
        local cx, cy = viewport.X / 2, viewport.Y / 2

        if circle then
            circle.Visible = circleEnabled
            circle.Position = Vector2.new(cx, cy)
            circle.Radius = self.Config.CircleRadius
            circle.Color = self.Config.CircleColor
        end
        if crossH and crossV then
            local size, gap = self.Config.CrosshairSize, self.Config.CrosshairGap
            crossH.Visible, crossV.Visible = crosshairEnabled, crosshairEnabled
            crossH.From, crossH.To = Vector2.new(cx - gap - size, cy), Vector2.new(cx - gap, cy)
            crossV.From, crossV.To = Vector2.new(cx, cy - gap - size), Vector2.new(cx, cy - gap)
            crossH.Color, crossV.Color = self.Config.CrosshairColor, self.Config.CrosshairColor
        end
    end

    local function refreshDrawings()
        if not circleEnabled and not crosshairEnabled then
            disconnect("drawings")
            removeDrawings()
            return
        end

        if circleEnabled and not circle then
            circle = Drawing.new("Circle")
            circle.NumSides = 64
            circle.Thickness = 1
            circle.Filled = false
        end
        if not circleEnabled and circle then
            circle:Remove()
            circle = nil
        end

        if crosshairEnabled and not crossH then
            crossH = Drawing.new("Line")
            crossV = Drawing.new("Line")
            crossH.Thickness, crossV.Thickness = 1, 1
        end
        if not crosshairEnabled and crossH then
            crossH:Remove()
            crossV:Remove()
            crossH, crossV = nil, nil
        end

        if not connections.drawings then
            connections.drawings = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(renderDrawings)
        end
    end

    function self.SetCircle(state)
        if state and not drawingAvailable() then return false end
        circleEnabled = state and true or false
        refreshDrawings()
        return true
    end

    function self.SetCrosshair(state)
        if state and not drawingAvailable() then return false end
        crosshairEnabled = state and true or false
        refreshDrawings()
        return true
    end

    -- Restores lighting and removes every drawing. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        flags.Fullbright, flags.NoFog, flags.Time = false, false, false
        circleEnabled, crosshairEnabled = false, false
        restore()
        disconnect("lighting")
        disconnect("drawings")
        removeDrawings()
    end

    return self
end

return Visuals
end)()
local VisualsController = WorldVisuals.new({
    RunService = RunService,
    Workspace = Workspace,
    Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting"),
})
Backshot.Visuals = VisualsController
--
--
-- Extras: china hat, cursor, head dots (backshot_extras.lua)
local ExtrasModule = (function()
-- backshot_extras.lua
-- China hat, custom cursor and head dots, written from scratch on public Roblox APIs.
--
-- Usage:
--   local Extras = loadstring(readfile("backshot/backshot_extras.lua"))()
--   local extras = Extras.new({
--       Players = game:GetService("Players"),
--       RunService = game:GetService("RunService"),
--       Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace"),
--       UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService"),
--   })
--   extras.SetChinaHat(true)
--   extras.SetCursor(true)
--   extras.SetHeadDots(true)   -- needs Drawing; returns false if unavailable
--   extras.Unload()
--
-- The china hat and cursor exist only on your own client. Other players do not see them.
-- The hat is built from stacked cylinders, so no asset ids are used.
-- The cursor replaces the system mouse icon, so it only matters where a mouse exists.

local Extras = {}

-- Pure helpers, kept free of Roblox objects so they can be checked on their own.

-- Diameter of hat layer `index` (0 = widest, count - 1 = narrowest).
local function layerDiameter(index, count, baseDiameter)
    return baseDiameter * (1 - index / count)
end

-- Dot radius in pixels: shrinks with distance, clamped to [minRadius, maxRadius].
local function dotRadius(distance, scale, minRadius, maxRadius)
    if distance <= 0 then return maxRadius end
    return math.clamp(scale / distance, minRadius, maxRadius)
end

local function drawingAvailable()
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local probe = Drawing.new("Line")
        probe:Remove()
    end)
    return ok
end

function Extras.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local RunService = env.RunService
    local Workspace = env.Workspace
    local UserInputService = env.UserInputService

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local connections = {}

    self.Config = {
        HatLayers = 12,
        HatBaseDiameter = 3.4,
        HatHeight = 1.3,
        HatOffset = 0.55,
        HatTransparency = 0.2,
        HatColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 160),
        HatRainbow = false,

        CursorSize = 10,
        CursorColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        CursorRing = true,

        HeadDotMaxDistance = 1000,
        HeadDotScale = 800,
        Filter = function(player)
            return player ~= LocalPlayer
        end,
        HeadDotColor = function(player)
            return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        end,
    }

    local function disconnect(key)
        local connection = connections[key]
        if connection and connection.Connected then
            connection:Disconnect()
        end
        connections[key] = nil
    end

    -- China hat ----------------------------------------------------------

    local hatParts = {}

    local function destroyHat()
        for _, part in hatParts do
            part:Destroy()
        end
        table.clear(hatParts)
    end

    local function buildHat()
        destroyHat()
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end

        local count = math.clamp(math.floor(self.Config.HatLayers), 3, 40)
        local layerHeight = self.Config.HatHeight / count
        for index = 0, count - 1 do
            local diameter = layerDiameter(index, count, self.Config.HatBaseDiameter)
            local part = Instance.new("Part")
            part.Name = "BackshotHatLayer"
            part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
            part.Size = Vector3.new(layerHeight, diameter, diameter)
            part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            part.Anchored = true
            part.CanCollide = false
            part.CanQuery = false
            part.CanTouch = false
            part.CastShadow = false
            part.Massless = true
            part.Transparency = 1
            part.Parent = camera
            table.insert(hatParts, part)
        end
    end

    local function updateHat()
        local count = #hatParts
        if count == 0 then return end

        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        local color = self.Config.HatColor
        if self.Config.HatRainbow then
            color = Color3.fromHSV((os.clock() % 5) / 5, 1, 1)
        end

        local layerHeight = self.Config.HatHeight / count
        for index, part in ipairs(hatParts) do
            if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then
                local y = self.Config.HatOffset + (index - 0.5) * layerHeight
                part.Transparency = self.Config.HatTransparency
                part.Color = color
                -- Position only, so the hat stays level when the head tilts.
                part.CFrame = CFrame.new(head.Position + Vector3.new(0, y, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2)
            else
                part.Transparency = 1
            end
        end
    end

    function self.SetChinaHat(state)
        disconnect("hat")
        destroyHat()
        if state then
            buildHat()
            connections.hat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(updateHat)
        end
        return true
    end

    -- Rebuild after changing layers or size while the hat is on.
    function self.RebuildHat()
        if connections.hat then
            self.SetChinaHat(true)
        end
    end

    -- Cursor -------------------------------------------------------------

    local cursorGui, cursorDot, cursorRing
    local savedIconEnabled = nil

    local function destroyCursor()
        disconnect("cursor")
        if cursorGui then
            cursorGui:Destroy()
        end
        cursorGui, cursorDot, cursorRing = nil, nil, nil
        if savedIconEnabled ~= nil then
            UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = savedIconEnabled
            savedIconEnabled = nil
        end
    end

    local function guiParent()
        local ok, container = pcall(function()
            return gethui()
        end)
        if ok and container then return container end
        return LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    end

    local function buildCursor()
        local parent = guiParent()
        if not parent then return false end

        local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        gui.Name = "BackshotCursor"
        gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        gui.DisplayOrder = 10000
        gui.Parent = parent

        local ring = Instance.new("Frame")
        ring.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ring.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ring.Parent = gui
        local ringCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        ringCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        ringCorner.Parent = ring
        local ringStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        ringStroke.Thickness = 1.5
        ringStroke.Parent = ring

        local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
        dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dot.Parent = gui
        local dotCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        dotCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        dotCorner.Parent = dot

        cursorGui, cursorDot, cursorRing = gui, dot, ring
        return true
    end

    local function updateCursor()
        if not cursorGui then return end

        local location = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        local position = UDim2.fromOffset(location.X, location.Y)
        local size = self.Config.CursorSize
        local color = self.Config.CursorColor

        cursorDot.Position = position
        cursorDot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
        cursorDot.BackgroundColor3 = color

        cursorRing.Visible = self.Config.CursorRing
        cursorRing.Position = position
        cursorRing.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size * 2.4, size * 2.4)
        local stroke = cursorRing:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
        if stroke then stroke.Color = color end

        -- Games turn the system icon back on, so keep it off every frame.
        UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = false
    end

    function self.SetCursor(state)
        destroyCursor()
        if not state then return true end

        savedIconEnabled = UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled
        if not buildCursor() then
            UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = savedIconEnabled
            savedIconEnabled = nil
            return false
        end
        connections.cursor = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(updateCursor)
        return true
    end

    -- Head dots ----------------------------------------------------------

    local dots = {}

    local function removeDot(player)
        local dot = dots[player]
        if dot then
            dot:Remove()
            dots[player] = nil
        end
    end

    local function removeAllDots()
        for player in pairs(dots) do
            removeDot(player)
        end
    end

    local function renderDots()
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end

        for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
            local okFilter, allowed = pcall(self.Config.Filter, player)
            local character = player.Character
            local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local dot = dots[player]

            if okFilter and allowed and head and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
                local distance = (camera.CFrame.Position - head.Position).Magnitude
                local screen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position)

                if distance <= self.Config.HeadDotMaxDistance and screen.Z > 0 then
                    if not dot then
                        dot = Drawing.new("Circle")
                        dot.Filled = true
                        dot.NumSides = 24
                        dots[player] = dot
                    end
                    local okColor, color = pcall(self.Config.HeadDotColor, player)
                    if not okColor or typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then
                        color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    end
                    dot.Color = color
                    dot.Position = Vector2.new(screen.X, screen.Y)
                    dot.Radius = dotRadius(distance, self.Config.HeadDotScale, 3, 14)
                    dot.Visible = true
                elseif dot then
                    dot.Visible = false
                end
            elseif dot then
                dot.Visible = false
            end
        end
    end

    function self.SetHeadDots(state)
        if state and not drawingAvailable() then return false end

        disconnect("dots")
        disconnect("dotsLeave")
        removeAllDots()

        if state then
            connections.dots = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(renderDots)
            connections.dotsLeave = Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(removeDot)
        end
        return true
    end

    -- Removes everything this module created. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        self.SetChinaHat(false)
        self.SetCursor(false)
        self.SetHeadDots(false)
    end

    -- Exposed for isolated checks.
    self._internal = { layerDiameter = layerDiameter, dotRadius = dotRadius }

    return self
end

return Extras

end)()
local ExtrasController = ExtrasModule.new({
    Players = game:GetService("Players"),
    RunService = RunService,
    Workspace = Workspace,
    UserInputService = UserInputService,
})
Backshot.Extras = ExtrasController
--
-- Auto farm (backshot_farm.lua)
local FarmModule = (function()
local Farm = {}

-- Pure helpers, kept free of Roblox objects so they can be checked on their own.

local function coinish(name)
    local lower = string.lower(name)
    return string.find(lower, "coin", 1, true) ~= nil or string.find(lower, "cand", 1, true) ~= nil
end

local function bagFull(got, bagMax)
    return got >= bagMax
end

function Farm.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local RunService = env.RunService
    local Workspace = env.Workspace
    local ReplicatedStorage = env.ReplicatedStorage

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local connections = {}
    local coins = {}          -- [part] = true
    local partConnections = {} -- [part] = { connections }
    local highlights = {}
    local visited, attempts, targeted = {}, {}, {}
    local got = 0
    local farmEnabled, espEnabled = false, false
    local tracking = false
    local lastStep = 0
    local hudGui, hudLabel
    local hooked = {}

    self.Config = {
        -- Optional override: function(part) -> boolean. Leave nil to use the coin rules above.
        TargetFilter = nil,
        Interval = 0.6,
        MaxDistance = 600,
        BagMax = 50,
        StopWhenFull = true,
        UseTouchEvent = true,
        ShowHud = true,
    }

    local function defaultIsCoin(part)
        if part.Name == "Coin_Server" then return true end
        local parent = part.Parent
        if parent then
            local parentName = string.lower(parent.Name)
            if coinish(parentName) and string.find(parentName, "container", 1, true) then
                return true
            end
        end
        return coinish(part.Name) and part:FindFirstChildOfClass("TouchTransmitter") ~= nil
    end

    local function matches(part)
        local filter = self.Config.TargetFilter
        if typeof(filter) == "function" then
            local ok, result = pcall(filter, part)
            return ok and result and true or false
        end
        return defaultIsCoin(part)
    end

    local function myRoot()
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if root and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
            return root
        end
        return nil
    end

    -- Coin highlights -------------------------------------------------------

    local function setHighlight(part, on)
        local existing = highlights[part]
        if not on then
            if existing then
                existing:Destroy()
                highlights[part] = nil
            end
            return
        end
        if existing and existing.Parent then return end

        local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
        highlight.Name = "BackshotCoinEsp"
        highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 60)
        highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 150)
        highlight.FillTransparency = 0.4
        highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        highlight.Adornee = part
        highlight.Parent = part
        highlights[part] = highlight
    end

    -- Coin tracking ---------------------------------------------------------

    local function removeCoin(part)
        coins[part] = nil
        local list = partConnections[part]
        if list then
            for _, connection in list do
                connection:Disconnect()
            end
            partConnections[part] = nil
        end
        setHighlight(part, false)
        visited[part], attempts[part], targeted[part] = nil, nil, nil
    end

    local function markConsumed(part)
        visited[part] = true
        local when = targeted[part]
        if when and os.clock() - when < 2 then
            got += 1
        end
    end

    local function addCoin(part)
        if coins[part] or not matches(part) then return end
        coins[part] = true

        partConnections[part] = {
            part.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
                if child:IsA("TouchTransmitter") then
                    markConsumed(part)
                end
            end),
            part.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
                if not part.Parent then
                    removeCoin(part)
                end
            end),
        }

        if espEnabled then
            setHighlight(part, true)
        end
    end

    local function startTracking()
        if tracking then return end
        tracking = true

        for _, descendant in Workspace:GetDescendants() do
            if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                pcall(addCoin, descendant)
            end
        end
        connections.added = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
            if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                pcall(addCoin, descendant)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function stopTracking()
        if not tracking then return end
        tracking = false
        if connections.added then
            connections.added:Disconnect()
            connections.added = nil
        end
        for part in pairs(coins) do
            removeCoin(part)
        end
    end

    local function resetCount()
        got = 0
        table.clear(visited)
        table.clear(attempts)
        table.clear(targeted)
    end

    function self.ResetCount()
        resetCount()
    end

    function self.GetCount()
        return got
    end

    -- HUD -------------------------------------------------------------------

    local function destroyHud()
        if hudGui then
            hudGui:Destroy()
        end
        hudGui, hudLabel = nil, nil
    end

    local function updateHud()
        if not self.Config.ShowHud then
            destroyHud()
            return
        end

        if not hudGui or not hudGui.Parent then
            local ok, container = pcall(function()
                return gethui()
            end)
            local parent = (ok and container) or LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
            if not parent then return end

            hudGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            hudGui.Name = "BackshotFarmHud"
            hudGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
            hudGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
            hudGui.DisplayOrder = 19999
            hudGui.Parent = parent

            hudLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            hudLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
            hudLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 52)
            hudLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(190, 26)
            hudLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 22, 30)
            hudLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
            hudLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 60)
            hudLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            hudLabel.TextSize = 14
            hudLabel.Parent = hudGui
            local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
            corner.Parent = hudLabel
        end

        local full = bagFull(got, self.Config.BagMax)
        local text = string.format("Coins: %d / %d%s", got, self.Config.BagMax, full and "  FULL" or "")
        if hudLabel.Text ~= text then
            hudLabel.Text = text
        end
    end

    -- Farming ---------------------------------------------------------------

    local function touch(root, part)
        if not self.Config.UseTouchEvent then return end
        if typeof(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" then return end
        pcall(firetouchinterest, root, part, 0)
        pcall(firetouchinterest, root, part, 1)
    end

    local function step()
        if not farmEnabled then return end
        updateHud()

        if self.Config.StopWhenFull and bagFull(got, self.Config.BagMax) then return end

        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastStep < self.Config.Interval then return end

        local root = myRoot()
        if not root then return end

        local best, bestDistance = nil, self.Config.MaxDistance
        for part in pairs(coins) do
            if part.Parent and not visited[part] and (attempts[part] or 0) < 3 then
                local distance = (part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if distance <= bestDistance then
                    best, bestDistance = part, distance
                end
            end
        end
        if not best then return end

        lastStep = now
        attempts[best] = (attempts[best] or 0) + 1
        targeted[best] = now

        -- Land ON TOP of whatever is under the coin, not at the coin's own height: a coin
        -- sitting flush with a floor can otherwise put you half inside that floor.
        local landing = best.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LocalPlayer.Character }
        local hit = Workspace:Raycast(landing, Vector3.new(0, -20, 0), params)
        if hit then
            landing = hit.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
        end

        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(landing)
        touch(root, best)
    end

    function self.SetEnabled(state)
        farmEnabled = state and true or false

        if farmEnabled then
            startTracking()
            if not connections.step then
                connections.step = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(step)
            end
        else
            if connections.step then
                connections.step:Disconnect()
                connections.step = nil
            end
            destroyHud()
            if not espEnabled then
                stopTracking()
            end
        end
    end

    function self.SetCoinEsp(state)
        espEnabled = state and true or false

        if espEnabled then
            startTracking()
            for part in pairs(coins) do
                setHighlight(part, true)
            end
        else
            for part in pairs(coins) do
                setHighlight(part, false)
            end
            if not farmEnabled then
                stopTracking()
            end
        end
    end

    -- Reset the count when a new round starts.
    local function hookRound(remote)
        if hooked[remote] or not remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") or remote.Name ~= "RoundStart" then return end
        hooked[remote] = true
        table.insert(connections, remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(resetCount))
    end
    if ReplicatedStorage then
        for _, descendant in ReplicatedStorage:GetDescendants() do
            pcall(hookRound, descendant)
        end
        table.insert(connections, ReplicatedStorage.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
            pcall(hookRound, descendant)
        end))
    end

    -- Stops everything, removes highlights and the HUD. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        farmEnabled, espEnabled = false, false
        stopTracking()
        destroyHud()
        for key, connection in pairs(connections) do
            if typeof(connection) == "RBXScriptConnection" and connection.Connected then
                connection:Disconnect()
            end
            connections[key] = nil
        end
        table.clear(hooked)
    end

    -- Exposed for isolated checks.
    self._internal = { coinish = coinish, bagFull = bagFull }

    return self
end

return Farm
end)()
local FarmController = FarmModule.new({
    Players = game:GetService("Players"),
    RunService = RunService,
    Workspace = Workspace,
    ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"),
})
Backshot.Farm = FarmController
--
--
-- Build world (backshot_builder.lua)
local BuilderModule = (function()
local Builder = {}

local MAX_PARTS = 3000
local MAX_UNDO = 100
local SAVE_FOLDER = "backshot_builds"

local PALETTE = {
    Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
    Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30),
    Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 50),
    Color3.fromRGB(240, 150, 40),
    Color3.fromRGB(240, 220, 60),
    Color3.fromRGB(70, 200, 90),
    Color3.fromRGB(60, 140, 240),
    Color3.fromRGB(170, 90, 230),
}

-- Pure helpers, kept free of Roblox instances so they can be checked on their own.

local function isFinite(value)
    return type(value) == "number" and value == value and value ~= math.huge and value ~= -math.huge
end

local function snap(value, grid)
    if grid <= 0 then return value end
    return math.floor(value / grid + 0.5) * grid
end

local function snapVector(vector, grid)
    return Vector3.new(snap(vector.X, grid), snap(vector.Y, grid), snap(vector.Z, grid))
end

-- Turns any surface normal into the closest axis-aligned unit vector.
local function dominantNormal(normal)
    local ax, ay, az = math.abs(normal.X), math.abs(normal.Y), math.abs(normal.Z)
    if ax >= ay and ax >= az then
        return Vector3.new(normal.X > 0 and 1 or -1, 0, 0)
    elseif ay >= az then
        return Vector3.new(0, normal.Y > 0 and 1 or -1, 0)
    end
    return Vector3.new(0, 0, normal.Z > 0 and 1 or -1)
end

-- Snaps the two axes along the surface to the grid and keeps the axis across
-- the surface exact, so a placed block sits flush on what was hit.
local function surfacePoint(position, normal, grid)
    local snapped = snapVector(position, grid)
    return Vector3.new(
        normal.X ~= 0 and position.X or snapped.X,
        normal.Y ~= 0 and position.Y or snapped.Y,
        normal.Z ~= 0 and position.Z or snapped.Z
    )
end

-- Half the extent of a block along each world axis, in the direction of `normal`.
local function faceOffset(normal, extent)
    return Vector3.new(normal.X * extent.X / 2, normal.Y * extent.Y / 2, normal.Z * extent.Z / 2)
end

local function clampSize(size)
    return Vector3.new(
        math.clamp(size.X, 0.2, 512),
        math.clamp(size.Y, 0.2, 512),
        math.clamp(size.Z, 0.2, 512)
    )
end

local function sanitizeName(name)
    local cleaned = tostring(name or ""):gsub("[^%w_%-]", "")
    return cleaned
end

local function encodePart(part, origin)
    local relative = origin:ToObjectSpace(part.CFrame)
    local color = part.Color
    return {
        s = { part.Size.X, part.Size.Y, part.Size.Z },
        c = { relative:GetComponents() },
        k = {
            math.floor(color.R * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(color.G * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(color.B * 255 + 0.5),
        },
        m = part.Material.Name,
    }
end

-- Returns size, relative CFrame, colour, material, or nil if the entry is invalid.
local function decodeEntry(entry)
    if type(entry) ~= "table" then return nil end
    local s, c, k = entry.s, entry.c, entry.k
    if type(s) ~= "table" or #s ~= 3 then return nil end
    if type(c) ~= "table" or #c ~= 12 then return nil end
    if type(k) ~= "table" or #k ~= 3 then return nil end
    if type(entry.m) ~= "string" then return nil end

    for _, value in s do
        if not isFinite(value) then return nil end
    end
    for _, value in c do
        if not isFinite(value) then return nil end
    end
    for _, value in k do
        if not isFinite(value) or value < 0 or value > 255 then return nil end
    end

    local okMaterial, material = pcall(function()
        return Enum.Material[entry.m]
    end)
    if not okMaterial or not material then return nil end

    return clampSize(Vector3.new(s[1], s[2], s[3])),
        CFrame.new(table.unpack(c)):Orthonormalize(),
        Color3.fromRGB(k[1], k[2], k[3]),
        material
end

function Builder.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local RunService = env.RunService
    local Workspace = env.Workspace
    local UserInputService = env.UserInputService
    local HttpService = env.HttpService

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local connections = {}
    local active = false
    local savedCFrame = nil
    local platform, world
    local toolbar = nil
    local modeButtons = {}
    local colorButton = nil
    local mode = "Place"
    local selected, selectionBox
    local undoStack = {}
    local colorIndex = 1
    local lastClear = 0

    self.Config = {
        Center = Vector3.new(0, 3000, 0),
        PlatformSize = Vector3.new(400, 4, 400),
        Grid = 1,
        BlockSize = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4),
        Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic,
        SaveName = "build1",
    }

    -- Set this to show messages (for example through a notification).
    self.OnMessage = function(text) end

    local function say(text)
        pcall(self.OnMessage, text)
    end

    local function disconnect(key)
        local connection = connections[key]
        if connection and connection.Connected then
            connection:Disconnect()
        end
        connections[key] = nil
    end

    local function getRoot()
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        return character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    local function guiParent()
        local ok, container = pcall(function()
            return gethui()
        end)
        if ok and container then return container end
        return LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    end

    local function origin()
        return CFrame.new(self.Config.Center)
    end

    local function ensureWorld()
        if not world or not world.Parent then
            world = Instance.new("Folder")
            world.Name = "BackshotBuildWorld"
            world.Parent = Workspace
        end
        if not platform or not platform.Parent then
            platform = Instance.new("Part")
            platform.Name = "BackshotBuildPlatform"
            platform.Anchored = true
            platform.CanCollide = true
            platform.Size = self.Config.PlatformSize
            platform.CFrame = origin()
            platform.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            platform.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 100)
            platform.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
            platform.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
            platform.Parent = Workspace
        end
    end

    local function pushUndo(action)
        table.insert(undoStack, action)
        if #undoStack > MAX_UNDO then
            table.remove(undoStack, 1)
        end
    end

    local function createBlock(size, cframe, color, material)
        ensureWorld()
        if #world:GetChildren() >= MAX_PARTS then return nil end

        local part = Instance.new("Part")
        part.Name = "Block"
        part.Anchored = true
        part.Size = size
        part.CFrame = cframe
        part.Color = color
        part.Material = material
        part.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
        part.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
        part.Parent = world
        return part
    end

    local function isBlock(instance)
        return instance ~= nil and instance.Parent == world and instance:IsA("BasePart")
    end

    local function setSelected(part)
        selected = part
        if selectionBox then
            selectionBox:Destroy()
            selectionBox = nil
        end
        if part then
            selectionBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
            selectionBox.Adornee = part
            selectionBox.LineThickness = 0.05
            selectionBox.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
            selectionBox.Parent = world
        end
    end

    -- Bounding extent of a part along the world axes, correct for any rotation.
    local function worldExtent(part)
        local rotation = part.CFrame.Rotation
        local x = rotation:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(part.Size.X, 0, 0))
        local y = rotation:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0, part.Size.Y, 0))
        local z = rotation:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0, 0, part.Size.Z))
        return Vector3.new(
            math.abs(x.X) + math.abs(y.X) + math.abs(z.X),
            math.abs(x.Y) + math.abs(y.Y) + math.abs(z.Y),
            math.abs(x.Z) + math.abs(y.Z) + math.abs(z.Z)
        )
    end

    local function raycastFromScreen(x, y)
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return nil end
        local ray = camera:ScreenPointToRay(x, y)
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { world, platform }
        return Workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 2000, params)
    end

    -- Tools ----------------------------------------------------------------

    local function useTool(result)
        local hit = result.Instance
        local normal = dominantNormal(result.Normal)
        local grid = self.Config.Grid

        if mode == "Place" then
            local size = self.Config.BlockSize
            local position = surfacePoint(result.Position, normal, grid) + faceOffset(normal, size)
            local part = createBlock(size, CFrame.new(position), PALETTE[colorIndex], self.Config.Material)
            if not part then
                say("Build: part limit reached")
                return
            end
            pushUndo(function()
                if part.Parent then part:Destroy() end
            end)

        elseif mode == "Delete" then
            if not isBlock(hit) then return end
            local size, cframe, color, material = hit.Size, hit.CFrame, hit.Color, hit.Material
            if selected == hit then setSelected(nil) end
            hit:Destroy()
            pushUndo(function()
                createBlock(size, cframe, color, material)
            end)

        elseif mode == "Color" then
            if not isBlock(hit) then return end
            local previous = hit.Color
            hit.Color = PALETTE[colorIndex]
            pushUndo(function()
                if hit.Parent then hit.Color = previous end
            end)

        elseif mode == "Move" then
            if not selected or not selected.Parent then
                if isBlock(hit) then setSelected(hit) end
                return
            end
            if hit == selected then
                setSelected(nil)
                return
            end
            local target = selected
            local previous = target.CFrame
