            local position = surfacePoint(result.Position, normal, grid) + faceOffset(normal, worldExtent(target))
            target.CFrame = CFrame.new(position) * (previous - previous.Position)
            pushUndo(function()
                if target.Parent then target.CFrame = previous end
            end)

        elseif mode == "Size" then
            if isBlock(hit) then
                setSelected(hit)
            else
                setSelected(nil)
            end
        end
    end

    local function onInput(input, processed)
        if processed or not active then return end
        local inputType = input.UserInputType
        if inputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and inputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            return
        end

        local result = raycastFromScreen(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        if not result then
            if mode == "Move" or mode == "Size" then setSelected(nil) end
            return
        end
        useTool(result)
    end

    local function adjustSize(axis, direction)
        local step = math.max(self.Config.Grid, 0.2)
        local delta = Vector3.new(
            axis == "X" and step or 0,
            axis == "Y" and step or 0,
            axis == "Z" and step or 0
        ) * direction

        if selected and selected.Parent then
            local target = selected
            local previous = target.Size
            target.Size = clampSize(previous + delta)
            pushUndo(function()
                if target.Parent then target.Size = previous end
            end)
        else
            self.Config.BlockSize = clampSize(self.Config.BlockSize + delta)
            local size = self.Config.BlockSize
            say(string.format("Block size %.1f x %.1f x %.1f", size.X, size.Y, size.Z))
        end
    end

    function self.Undo()
        local action = table.remove(undoStack)
        if action then
            action()
        else
            say("Build: nothing to undo")
        end
    end

    function self.Rotate()
        if not selected or not selected.Parent then
            say("Build: select a block first (Move or Size tool)")
            return
        end
        local target = selected
        local previous = target.CFrame
        target.CFrame = previous * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi / 2, 0)
        pushUndo(function()
            if target.Parent then target.CFrame = previous end
        end)
    end

    function self.NextColor()
        colorIndex = colorIndex % #PALETTE + 1
        if colorButton then
            colorButton.BackgroundColor3 = PALETTE[colorIndex]
            colorButton.TextColor3 = colorIndex == 2 and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end
    end

    function self.SetMode(newMode)
        mode = newMode
        if mode == "Place" or mode == "Delete" or mode == "Color" then
            setSelected(nil)
        end
        for name, button in pairs(modeButtons) do
            button.BackgroundColor3 = name == mode and Color3.fromRGB(60, 140, 240) or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 50)
        end
    end

    function self.Clear()
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastClear > 2 then
            lastClear = now
            say("Build: tap Clear again to delete everything")
            return
        end
        lastClear = 0
        setSelected(nil)
        if world then world:ClearAllChildren() end
        table.clear(undoStack)
        say("Build: cleared")
    end

    -- Save / load ----------------------------------------------------------

    local function filesAvailable()
        return typeof(writefile) == "function" and typeof(readfile) == "function"
            and typeof(isfile) == "function" and typeof(makefolder) == "function"
    end

    function self.Save(name)
        local fileName = sanitizeName(name or self.Config.SaveName)
        if fileName == "" then
            say("Build: invalid save name")
            return false
        end
        if not filesAvailable() then
            say("Build: this executor has no file functions")
            return false
        end
        ensureWorld()

        local entries = {}
        for _, child in world:GetChildren() do
            if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                table.insert(entries, encodePart(child, origin()))
            end
        end

        local okEncode, json = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONEncode({ version = 1, parts = entries })
        end)
        if not okEncode then
            say("Build: could not encode the build")
            return false
        end

        local okWrite = pcall(function()
            if typeof(isfolder) ~= "function" or not isfolder(SAVE_FOLDER) then
                makefolder(SAVE_FOLDER)
            end
            writefile(SAVE_FOLDER .. "/" .. fileName .. ".json", json)
        end)
        if not okWrite then
            say("Build: could not write the file")
            return false
        end

        say(string.format("Build: saved %d blocks as %s", #entries, fileName))
        return true
    end

    function self.Load(name)
        local fileName = sanitizeName(name or self.Config.SaveName)
        if fileName == "" then
            say("Build: invalid save name")
            return false
        end
        if not filesAvailable() then
            say("Build: this executor has no file functions")
            return false
        end

        local path = SAVE_FOLDER .. "/" .. fileName .. ".json"
        local okExists, exists = pcall(isfile, path)
        if not okExists or not exists then
            say("Build: no save named " .. fileName)
            return false
        end

        local okRead, raw = pcall(readfile, path)
        if not okRead then
            say("Build: could not read the file")
            return false
        end
        local okDecode, data = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw)
        end)
        if not okDecode or type(data) ~= "table" or type(data.parts) ~= "table" then
            say("Build: the save file is not valid")
            return false
        end

        ensureWorld()
        setSelected(nil)
        world:ClearAllChildren()
        table.clear(undoStack)

        local loaded, skipped = 0, 0
        for _, entry in data.parts do
            local size, relative, color, material = decodeEntry(entry)
            if size then
                local part = createBlock(size, origin() * relative, color, material)
                if part then
                    loaded += 1
                else
                    skipped += 1
                    break
                end
            else
                skipped += 1
            end
        end

        say(string.format("Build: loaded %d blocks, skipped %d", loaded, skipped))
        return true
    end

    -- Toolbar --------------------------------------------------------------

    local function buildToolbar()
        local parent = guiParent()
        if not parent then return false end

        local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        gui.Name = "BackshotBuilder"
        gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        gui.DisplayOrder = 9000
        gui.Parent = parent

        local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
        frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
        frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -6)
        frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6 * 62 + 8, 3 * 32 + 8)
        frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 24)
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
        frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        frame.Parent = gui

        local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
        padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4)
        padding.Parent = frame

        local layout = Instance.new("UIGridLayout")
        layout.CellSize = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 28)
        layout.CellPadding = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4)
        layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        layout.Parent = frame

        local specs = {
            { "Place", function() self.SetMode("Place") end, "Place" },
            { "Move", function() self.SetMode("Move") end, "Move" },
            { "Size", function() self.SetMode("Size") end, "Size" },
            { "Color", function() self.SetMode("Color") end, "Color" },
            { "Del", function() self.SetMode("Delete") end, "Delete" },
            { "Exit", function() self.Exit() end },
            { "Undo", function() self.Undo() end },
            { "Rot", function() self.Rotate() end },
            { "Save", function() self.Save() end },
            { "Load", function() self.Load() end },
            { "Clear", function() self.Clear() end },
            { "Col", function() self.NextColor() end, nil, true },
            { "X+", function() adjustSize("X", 1) end },
            { "X-", function() adjustSize("X", -1) end },
            { "Y+", function() adjustSize("Y", 1) end },
            { "Y-", function() adjustSize("Y", -1) end },
            { "Z+", function() adjustSize("Z", 1) end },
            { "Z-", function() adjustSize("Z", -1) end },
        }

        table.clear(modeButtons)
        for order, spec in ipairs(specs) do
            local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
            button.LayoutOrder = order
            button.Text = spec[1]
            button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            button.TextSize = 12
            button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
            button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 50)
            button.BorderSizePixel = 0
            button.AutoButtonColor = true
            button.Parent = frame
            button.Activated:Connect(spec[2])

            if spec[3] then
                modeButtons[spec[3]] = button
            end
            if spec[4] then
                colorButton = button
                button.BackgroundColor3 = PALETTE[colorIndex]
                button.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end
        end

        toolbar = gui
        return true
    end

    -- Prefabs: a few ready-made structures, stamped at your current position so you can
    -- keep editing them with the normal tools afterwards. Built from plain blocks, same as
    -- everything you place by hand.
    self.PrefabNames = { "Tower", "Bridge", "Room", "Stairs" }

    local function stampBlock(size, localOffset, baseCFrame, color, material)
        createBlock(size, baseCFrame * CFrame.new(localOffset), color or PALETTE[colorIndex], material or self.Config.Material)
    end

    local PREFABS = {
        Tower = function(baseCFrame)
            for level = 0, 7 do
                stampBlock(Vector3.new(6, 2, 6), Vector3.new(0, level * 2 + 1, 0), baseCFrame)
            end
        end,
        Bridge = function(baseCFrame)
            for i = 0, 9 do
                stampBlock(Vector3.new(4, 1, 4), Vector3.new(0, 0, i * 4), baseCFrame)
            end
            for i = 0, 9 do
                stampBlock(Vector3.new(1, 3, 4), Vector3.new(-2, 2, i * 4), baseCFrame)
                stampBlock(Vector3.new(1, 3, 4), Vector3.new(2, 2, i * 4), baseCFrame)
            end
        end,
        Room = function(baseCFrame)
            local size, wall = 16, 1
            stampBlock(Vector3.new(size, 1, size), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), baseCFrame)
            stampBlock(Vector3.new(size, 8, wall), Vector3.new(0, 4.5, size / 2), baseCFrame)
            stampBlock(Vector3.new(size, 8, wall), Vector3.new(0, 4.5, -size / 2), baseCFrame)
            stampBlock(Vector3.new(wall, 8, size), Vector3.new(size / 2, 4.5, 0), baseCFrame)
            stampBlock(Vector3.new(wall, 8, size), Vector3.new(-size / 2, 4.5, 0), baseCFrame)
        end,
        Stairs = function(baseCFrame)
            for step = 0, 9 do
                stampBlock(Vector3.new(4, 1, 2), Vector3.new(0, step + 0.5, step * 2), baseCFrame)
            end
        end,
    }

    function self.StampPrefab(name)
        local builder = PREFABS[name]
        if not builder then
            say("Build: unknown prefab " .. tostring(name))
            return false
        end
        ensureWorld()

        local root = getRoot()
        local look = root and root.CFrame.LookVector or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
        local flatLook = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
        if flatLook.Magnitude < 0.01 then flatLook = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
        local center = root and root.Position or self.Config.Center
        local baseCFrame = CFrame.new(center + flatLook.Unit * 6, center + flatLook.Unit * 12 + Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
        baseCFrame = CFrame.new(baseCFrame.Position) * (baseCFrame - baseCFrame.Position)

        builder(baseCFrame)
        pushUndo(function() end) -- prefabs are many blocks; Clear (double tap) removes them, not single Undo
        say("Build: placed " .. name)
        return true
    end

    -- Enter / exit ---------------------------------------------------------

    function self.Enter()
        if active then return true end

        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not (root and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0) then
            return false
        end

        ensureWorld()
        if not buildToolbar() then
            return false
        end

        savedCFrame = root.CFrame
        active = true
        self.SetMode("Place")

        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(self.Config.Center + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0))
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

        connections.input = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(onInput)
        connections.heartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local currentCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
            local currentRoot = currentCharacter and currentCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local currentHumanoid = currentCharacter and currentCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not currentRoot or not currentHumanoid or currentHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
                self.Exit()
                return
            end

            local offset = currentRoot.Position - self.Config.Center
            if currentRoot.Position.Y < self.Config.Center.Y - 30
                or math.abs(offset.X) > 300 or math.abs(offset.Z) > 300 then
                currentRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(self.Config.Center + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0))
                currentRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end
        end)

        say("Build world: tap the world to use the tool")
        return true
    end

    function self.Exit()
        if not active then return end
        active = false

        disconnect("input")
        disconnect("heartbeat")
        if toolbar then
            toolbar:Destroy()
            toolbar = nil
        end
        table.clear(modeButtons)
        colorButton = nil
        setSelected(nil)

        local root = getRoot()
        if root and savedCFrame then
            root.CFrame = savedCFrame
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end
        savedCFrame = nil
    end

    function self.IsActive()
        return active
    end

    -- Takes you back, and removes the platform and everything built. Safe to call twice.
    function self.Unload()
        self.Exit()
        if world then
            world:Destroy()
            world = nil
        end
        if platform then
            platform:Destroy()
            platform = nil
        end
        table.clear(undoStack)
    end

    -- Exposed for isolated checks.
    self._internal = {
        snap = snap,
        surfacePoint = surfacePoint,
        dominantNormal = dominantNormal,
        faceOffset = faceOffset,
        sanitizeName = sanitizeName,
        isFinite = isFinite,
        decodeEntry = decodeEntry,
    }

    return self
end

return Builder
end)()
local BuilderController = BuilderModule.new({
    Players = game:GetService("Players"),
    RunService = RunService,
    Workspace = Workspace,
    UserInputService = UserInputService,
    HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService"),
})
Backshot.Builder = BuilderController
--

--
-- Silent aim, kill aura, kill effects, kill all (backshot_silentaim.lua)
local SilentAimModule = (function()
local SilentAim = {}

-- Pure helper, kept free of Roblox objects so it can be checked on its own.
-- Returns true if `now` is far enough past `lastTime` for another hit on this target.
local function offCooldown(now, lastTime, cooldown)
    return (now - (lastTime or -math.huge)) >= cooldown
end

function SilentAim.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local RunService = env.RunService
    local Workspace = env.Workspace
    local UserInputService = env.UserInputService

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local connections = {}
    local silentEnabled, auraEnabled, effectsEnabled = false, false, false
    local lastHit = {}

    self.OnMessage = function(text) end

    self.Config = {
        -- Optional overrides. Leave nil to use the knife remote described at the top.
        FireRemote = nil,            -- a RemoteEvent instance
        BuildArgs = nil,             -- function(player) -> table of arguments for FireServer
        HitHead = true,              -- with the default remote, also hit the head right after
        KillAllIgnoresRange = true,  -- Kill all targets everyone alive, not only players in Range
        KillEffectStyle = "Explosion",  -- "Explosion", "Ring", "Beam", "Confetti", "Dark"
        KillEffectColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60),

        Range = 20,                  -- studs, for silent aim and kill aura target search
        HitCooldown = 0.5,           -- seconds between attempts on the same target
        Key = Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1,  -- silent aim trigger
        Filter = function(player)
            return player ~= LocalPlayer
        end,
    }

    local function say(text)
        pcall(self.OnMessage, text)
    end

    local function track(connection)
        table.insert(connections, connection)
        return connection
    end

    local function myRoot()
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        return character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    -- Every player within Config.Range that passes Config.Filter and is alive.
    local function nearbyTargets(ignoreRange)
        local root = myRoot()
        if not root then return {} end

        local list = {}
        for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
            local okFilter, allowed = pcall(self.Config.Filter, player)
            local character = player.Character
            local targetRoot = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if okFilter and allowed and targetRoot and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
                local distance = (targetRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if ignoreRange or distance <= self.Config.Range then
                    table.insert(list, player)
                end
            end
        end
        return list
    end

    local lastWarn = 0
    local function warnOnce(text)
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastWarn >= 5 then
            lastWarn = now
            say(text)
        end
    end

    -- Knife.Events.HandleTouched in your Character or Backpack (name from the Zenith source).
    local function findKnifeRemote()
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        local knife = (character and character:FindFirstChild("Knife")) or (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Knife"))
        local events = knife and knife:FindFirstChild("Events")
        return events and events:FindFirstChild("HandleTouched") or nil
    end

    local function defaultBuildArgs(target)
        local character = target.Character
        local part = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not part then return nil end
        return { part }
    end

    -- The one place that actually sends a hit. Everything else just decides who and when.
    function self.FireKill(player)
        local remote = self.Config.FireRemote
        if typeof(remote) ~= "Instance" then
            remote = findKnifeRemote()
        end
        if not remote then
            warnOnce("Kill: no knife found (only the murderer has one)")
            return false
        end

        local now = os.clock()
        if not offCooldown(now, lastHit[player], self.Config.HitCooldown) then
            return false
        end

        local builder = self.Config.BuildArgs
        local usingDefault = typeof(builder) ~= "function"
        if usingDefault then
            builder = defaultBuildArgs
        end

        local okArgs, args = pcall(builder, player)
        if not okArgs or type(args) ~= "table" then
            return false
        end

        local okFire = pcall(function()
            remote:FireServer(table.unpack(args))
        end)
        if not okFire then
            return false
        end
        lastHit[player] = now

        if usingDefault and self.Config.HitHead then
            task.delay(0.03, function()
                local character = player.Character
                local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if head and remote.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        remote:FireServer(head)
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
        return true
    end

    -- Silent aim: on key press, fires at the closest valid target in range. No camera movement.
    local function onInputBegan(input, processed)
        if processed or not silentEnabled then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= self.Config.Key then return end

        local targets = nearbyTargets()
        local root = myRoot()
        if not root or #targets == 0 then return end

        local closest, closestDistance = nil, math.huge
        for _, player in targets do
            local targetRoot = player.Character.HumanoidRootPart
            local distance = (targetRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
            if distance < closestDistance then
                closest, closestDistance = player, distance
            end
        end
        if closest then
            self.FireKill(closest)
        end
    end

    function self.SetSilentAim(state)
        silentEnabled = state and true or false
        if silentEnabled and not connections.silentInput then
            connections.silentInput = track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(onInputBegan))
        elseif not silentEnabled and connections.silentInput then
            connections.silentInput:Disconnect()
            connections.silentInput = nil
        end
    end

    -- Kill aura: every frame, tries every valid target in range (HitCooldown prevents spam).
    local function onAuraHeartbeat()
        if not auraEnabled then return end
        for _, player in nearbyTargets() do
            self.FireKill(player)
        end
    end

    function self.SetKillAura(state)
        auraEnabled = state and true or false
        if auraEnabled and not connections.aura then
            connections.aura = track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(onAuraHeartbeat))
        elseif not auraEnabled and connections.aura then
            connections.aura:Disconnect()
            connections.aura = nil
        end
    end

    -- Kill-all: one pass over every currently valid target, right now.
    function self.KillAll()
        local targets = nearbyTargets(self.Config.KillAllIgnoresRange)
        local sent = 0
        for _, player in targets do
            if self.FireKill(player) then
                sent += 1
            end
        end
        say(string.format("Kill all: attempted %d of %d targets", sent, #targets))
        return sent
    end

    -- Kill effects: a local-only visual burst wherever a tracked humanoid dies.
    -- This needs no remote and always works, because it only reacts to Humanoid.Died.
    local deathConnections = {}

    self.KillEffectStyles = { "Explosion", "Ring", "Beam", "Confetti", "Dark" }

    local function sphereBurst(position, color, size)
        local part = Instance.new("Part")
        part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
        part.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
        part.Anchored = true
        part.CanCollide = false
        part.CanQuery = false
        part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        part.Color = color
        part.Position = position
        part.Parent = Workspace

        local tween = game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(
            part,
            TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            { Size = Vector3.new(size, size, size), Transparency = 1 }
        )
        tween:Play()
        tween.Completed:Connect(function() part:Destroy() end)
    end

    local function ringBurst(position, color)
        local part = Instance.new("Part")
        part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
        part.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 2, 2)
        part.Anchored = true
        part.CanCollide = false
        part.CanQuery = false
        part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        part.Color = color
        part.CFrame = CFrame.new(position) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2)
        part.Transparency = 0.2
        part.Parent = Workspace

        local tween = game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(
            part,
            TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            { Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 14, 14), Transparency = 1 }
        )
        tween:Play()
        tween.Completed:Connect(function() part:Destroy() end)
    end

    local function beamBurst(position, color)
        local bottom = Instance.new("Attachment")
        local anchor = Instance.new("Part")
        anchor.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
        anchor.Anchored = true
        anchor.CanCollide = false
        anchor.CanQuery = false
        anchor.Transparency = 1
        anchor.Position = position
        anchor.Parent = Workspace
        bottom.Parent = anchor

        local top = Instance.new("Attachment")
        local anchorTop = anchor:Clone()
        anchorTop.Position = position + Vector3.new(0, 30, 0)
        anchorTop.Parent = Workspace
        top.Parent = anchorTop

        local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
        beam.Attachment0 = bottom
        beam.Attachment1 = top
        beam.Width0, beam.Width1 = 2, 0.2
        beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(color)
        beam.FaceCamera = true
        beam.Parent = anchor

        task.delay(0.6, function()
            anchor:Destroy()
            anchorTop:Destroy()
        end)
    end

    local function confettiBurst(position, color)
        local part = Instance.new("Part")
        part.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
        part.Anchored = true
        part.CanCollide = false
        part.CanQuery = false
        part.Transparency = 1
        part.Position = position
        part.Parent = Workspace

        local particles = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        particles.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
        particles.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.5, 1)
        particles.Speed = NumberRange.new(6, 14)
        particles.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180)
        particles.Rate = 0
        particles.Color = ColorSequence.new(color)
        particles.Parent = part
        particles:Emit(40)

        task.delay(1.5, function() part:Destroy() end)
    end

    local function darkBurst(position, color)
        local part = Instance.new("Part")
        part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
        part.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)
        part.Anchored = true
        part.CanCollide = false
        part.CanQuery = false
        part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 15)
        part.Position = position
        part.Parent = Workspace

        local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
        light.Color = color
        light.Range = 12
        light.Brightness = 2
        light.Parent = part

        local tween = game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(
            part,
            TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
            { Size = Vector3.new(10, 10, 10), Transparency = 1 }
        )
        tween:Play()
        tween.Completed:Connect(function() part:Destroy() end)
    end

    local function playEffect(position)
        local color = self.Config.KillEffectColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
        local style = self.Config.KillEffectStyle or "Explosion"
        if style == "Ring" then
            ringBurst(position, color)
        elseif style == "Beam" then
            beamBurst(position, color)
        elseif style == "Confetti" then
            confettiBurst(position, color)
        elseif style == "Dark" then
            darkBurst(position, color)
        else
            sphereBurst(position, color, 8)
        end
    end

    local function watchHumanoid(player, character)
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not humanoid or not root then return end

        local connection = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
            if effectsEnabled then
                playEffect(root.Position)
            end
        end)
        deathConnections[player] = connection
    end

    local function onCharacterAdded(player, character)
        if deathConnections[player] then
            deathConnections[player]:Disconnect()
            deathConnections[player] = nil
        end
        watchHumanoid(player, character)
    end

    function self.SetKillEffects(state)
        effectsEnabled = state and true or false
    end

    -- Wire character tracking for every current and future player, once.
    for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
        if player.Character then
            onCharacterAdded(player, player.Character)
        end
        track(player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
            onCharacterAdded(player, character)
        end))
    end
    track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
        track(player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
            onCharacterAdded(player, character)
        end))
    end))
    track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
        if deathConnections[player] then
            deathConnections[player]:Disconnect()
            deathConnections[player] = nil
        end
        lastHit[player] = nil
    end))

    -- Turns everything off and disconnects every connection. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        silentEnabled, auraEnabled, effectsEnabled = false, false, false
        for _, connection in connections do
            if connection.Connected then
                connection:Disconnect()
            end
        end
        table.clear(connections)
        for _, connection in pairs(deathConnections) do
            connection:Disconnect()
        end
        table.clear(deathConnections)
        table.clear(lastHit)
    end

    -- Exposed for isolated checks.
    self._internal = { offCooldown = offCooldown }

    return self
end

return SilentAim
end)()
local SilentAimController = SilentAimModule.new({
    Players = Players,
    RunService = RunService,
    Workspace = Workspace,
    UserInputService = UserInputService,
})
Backshot.SilentAim = SilentAimController
--
--
-- Role detection (backshot_roles.lua)
local RolesModule = (function()
-- backshot_roles.lua
-- Role detection for Murder Mystery 2 players, written from scratch.
--
-- Where the data comes from (names taken from the Zenith and Candy source files you
-- sent; I have NOT verified them on your current game build):
--   1. Remotes anywhere under ReplicatedStorage:
--        RemoteEvent    "PlayerDataChanged"  server sends a table keyed by player name (or Player),
--                                            each value a table with Role / role and sometimes Dead / Killed
--        RemoteFunction "GetPlayerData"      returns the same table when invoked once
--        RemoteEvent    "LoadingMap"         a new round is starting, roles are cleared
--   2. Fallback when no data was received: a player holding a tool named "Knife" is the
--      murderer, a player holding a tool named "Gun" is the sheriff (Backpack or Character).
-- If neither source works on your build, GetRole returns "Unknown" and nothing breaks.
--
-- Usage:
--   local Roles = loadstring(readfile("backshot/backshot_roles.lua"))()
--   local roles = Roles.new({
--       Players = game:GetService("Players"),
--       ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"),
--   })
--   roles.GetRole(player)        --> "Murderer" | "Sheriff" | "Hero" | "Innocent" | "Unknown" | other string
--   roles.GetColor(player)       --> Color3
--   roles.FindByRole("Murderer") --> Player or nil
--   roles.Unload()

local Roles = {}

-- Pure helper, kept free of Roblox objects so it can be checked on its own.
-- Known roles get a fixed spelling, any other string is passed through, non-strings give nil.
local function normalizeRole(role)
    if type(role) ~= "string" then return nil end
    local lower = string.lower(role)
    if lower == "murderer" then return "Murderer" end
    if lower == "sheriff" then return "Sheriff" end
    if lower == "hero" then return "Hero" end
    if lower == "innocent" then return "Innocent" end
    return role
end

function Roles.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local ReplicatedStorage = env.ReplicatedStorage

    local self = {}
    local roles, dead = {}, {}
    local connections = {}
    local hooked = {}
    local dataFunction = nil

    self.Config = {
        UseTools = true,
        Colors = {
            Murderer = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60),
            Sheriff = Color3.fromRGB(60, 130, 255),
            Hero = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 60),
            Innocent = Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 120),
            Unknown = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        },
    }

    local function readData(tbl)
        if typeof(tbl) ~= "table" then return end
        for key, value in pairs(tbl) do
            local player = nil
            if typeof(key) == "Instance" and key:IsA("Player") then
                player = key
            else
                player = Players:FindFirstChild(tostring(key))
            end

            if player and typeof(value) == "table" then
                local role = normalizeRole(value.Role or value.role)
                if role then
                    roles[player] = role
                end
                dead[player] = (value.Dead == true or value.Killed == true) or nil
            end
        end
    end

    local function hookRemote(remote)
        if hooked[remote] then return end

        if remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            if remote.Name == "PlayerDataChanged" then
                hooked[remote] = true
                table.insert(connections, remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
                    readData((...))
                end))
            elseif remote.Name == "LoadingMap" then
                hooked[remote] = true
                table.insert(connections, remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
                    table.clear(roles)
                    table.clear(dead)
                end))
            end
        elseif remote:IsA("RemoteFunction") and remote.Name == "GetPlayerData" then
            hooked[remote] = true
            dataFunction = remote
            task.spawn(function()
                local ok, result = pcall(function()
                    return remote:InvokeServer()
                end)
                if ok then readData(result) end
            end)
        end
    end

    -- Asks the server for the current role table again (the game's own GetPlayerData call).
    function self.Refresh()
        local remote = dataFunction
        if not remote or not remote.Parent then return end
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, result = pcall(function()
                return remote:InvokeServer()
            end)
            if ok then readData(result) end
        end)
    end

    function self.GetRole(player)
        local known = roles[player]
        if known then return known end

        if self.Config.UseTools then
            local character = player.Character
            local backpack = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            if (character and character:FindFirstChild("Knife")) or (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Knife")) then
                return "Murderer"
            end
            if (character and character:FindFirstChild("Gun")) or (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Gun")) then
                return "Sheriff"
            end
        end
        return "Unknown"
    end

    function self.GetColor(player)
        local colors = self.Config.Colors
        return colors[self.GetRole(player)] or colors.Unknown
    end

    function self.IsDead(player)
        return dead[player] == true
    end

    -- First player with this role. Asking for "Sheriff" also matches "Hero".
    function self.FindByRole(wanted)
        for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
            local role = self.GetRole(player)
            if role == wanted or (wanted == "Sheriff" and role == "Hero") then
                return player
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    for _, descendant in ReplicatedStorage:GetDescendants() do
        pcall(hookRemote, descendant)
    end
    table.insert(connections, ReplicatedStorage.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
        pcall(hookRemote, descendant)
    end))
    table.insert(connections, Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
        roles[player] = nil
        dead[player] = nil
    end))

    -- Disconnects everything and clears the role table. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        for _, connection in connections do
            if connection.Connected then
                connection:Disconnect()
            end
        end
        table.clear(connections)
        table.clear(roles)
        table.clear(dead)
        table.clear(hooked)
        dataFunction = nil
    end

    -- Exposed for isolated checks.
    self._internal = { normalizeRole = normalizeRole }

    return self
end

return Roles

end)()
local RolesController = RolesModule.new({
    Players = Players,
    ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"),
})
Backshot.Roles = RolesController
--
-- Stand: follow a chosen player (backshot_stand.lua)
local StandModule = (function()
local Stand = {}

-- Poses: Sit / jojo / Aura / zby are Candy's frozen-frame poses (id + the exact time
-- position they freeze on). Wave / Point / Laugh / Cheer / Dance1-3 are MM2's own emotes,
-- read from your character's console dump earlier in this chat, so they play looped at
-- normal speed instead of being frozen.
local POSES = {
    Sit =   { id = 12507085924, freeze = true, time = 7 },
    jojo =  { id = 13823324057, freeze = true, time = 10 },
    Aura =  { id = 10478338114, freeze = true, time = 1 },
    zby =   { id = 10214311282, freeze = true, time = 3 },
    Wave =  { id = 507770239, freeze = false },
    Point = { id = 507770453, freeze = false },
    Laugh = { id = 507770818, freeze = false },
    Cheer = { id = 507770677, freeze = false },
    Dance1 = { id = 507771019, freeze = false },
    Dance2 = { id = 507776043, freeze = false },
    Dance3 = { id = 507777268, freeze = false },
    -- Sent by the user in chat; not verified by me in-game.
    Custom1 = { id = 13020180947, freeze = false },
}

Stand.PoseNames = { "None", "Sit", "jojo", "Aura", "zby", "Wave", "Point", "Laugh", "Cheer", "Dance1", "Dance2", "Dance3", "Custom1" }
-- Exposed so other modules (the Stand buddy) can reuse the same pose data instead of
-- duplicating these ids.
Stand.Poses = POSES

-- Pure helper: offset from the target for a side, elevation and distance, plus a manual
-- nudge (nudgeX = left/right, nudgeY = up/down, nudgeZ = forward/back) on top of it.
-- Distance 3 reproduces Candy's offsets: Left (-3, y, 2), Right (3, y, 2), Behind (0, y, 2).
local function computeOffset(side, elevation, distance, nudgeX, nudgeY, nudgeZ)
    local y = elevation == "Above" and 4 or 0.5
    local scale = distance / 3
    local base
    if side == "Right" then
        base = Vector3.new(3 * scale, y, 2 * scale)
    elseif side == "Behind" then
        base = Vector3.new(0, y, 2 * scale)
    else
        base = Vector3.new(-3 * scale, y, 2 * scale)
    end
    return base + Vector3.new(nudgeX or 0, nudgeY or 0, nudgeZ or 0)
end

function Stand.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local RunService = env.RunService

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local connections = {}
    local active = false
    local targetPlayer = nil
    local animationTrack = nil
    local freezeTime = 0

    self.Config = {
        Side = "Left",
        Elevation = "Level",
        Distance = 3,
        Pose = "None",
        -- Manual nudge on top of Side/Elevation/Distance: X = left(-)/right(+),
        -- Y = down(-)/up(+), Z = back(-)/forward(+), all in studs.
        NudgeX = 0,
        NudgeY = 0,
        NudgeZ = 0,
        -- Custom animation: used instead of the pose when set.
        AnimationId = nil,
        AnimationTime = 0,
    }

    local function getRoot(player)
        local character = player and player.Character
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if root and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
            return root, humanoid
        end
        return nil, nil
    end

    local function stopAnimation()
        if animationTrack then
            pcall(function() animationTrack:Stop() end)
            animationTrack = nil
        end
    end

    local shouldFreeze = false

    local function playAnimation(humanoid)
        stopAnimation()

        local id, time, freeze = self.Config.AnimationId, self.Config.AnimationTime, true
        if not id then
            local pose = POSES[self.Config.Pose]
            if not pose then return end
            id, time, freeze = pose.id, pose.time, pose.freeze
        end
        shouldFreeze = freeze and true or false

        local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
        if not animator then return end

        local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
        animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
        local okLoad, track = pcall(function()
            return animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
        end)
        if okLoad and track then
            animationTrack = track
            freezeTime = time or 0
            track.Looped = true
            track:Play()
            if shouldFreeze then
                track:AdjustSpeed(0)
                track.TimePosition = freezeTime
            end
        end
    end

    local function onHeartbeat()
        if not active then return end

        if not targetPlayer or not targetPlayer.Parent then
            self.Stop()
            return
        end

        local myRoot = getRoot(LocalPlayer)
        local targetRoot = getRoot(targetPlayer)
        if not myRoot or not targetRoot then return end

        myRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        local offset = computeOffset(
            self.Config.Side, self.Config.Elevation, self.Config.Distance,
            self.Config.NudgeX, self.Config.NudgeY, self.Config.NudgeZ
        )
        myRoot.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(offset)

        if animationTrack and shouldFreeze then
            animationTrack.TimePosition = freezeTime
        end
    end

    function self.Start(playerName)
        local player = Players:FindFirstChild(tostring(playerName))
        if not player or player == LocalPlayer then return false end

        targetPlayer = player
        active = true

        local _, myHumanoid = getRoot(LocalPlayer)
        if myHumanoid then
            playAnimation(myHumanoid)
        end

        if not connections.heartbeat then
            connections.heartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(onHeartbeat)
        end
        return true
    end

    function self.Stop()
        active = false
        targetPlayer = nil
        stopAnimation()
    end

    -- Changes the pose, also while the stand is running.
    function self.SetPose(name)
        self.Config.Pose = name
        if active then
            local _, humanoid = getRoot(LocalPlayer)
            if humanoid then
                playAnimation(humanoid)
            end
        end
    end

    -- Stops following and disconnects everything this module created. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        self.Stop()
        for key, connection in pairs(connections) do
            if connection.Connected then
                connection:Disconnect()
            end
            connections[key] = nil
        end
    end

    -- Exposed for isolated checks.
    self._internal = { computeOffset = computeOffset }

    return self
end

return Stand
end)()
local StandController = StandModule.new({
    Players = Players,
    RunService = RunService,
})
Backshot.Stand = StandController
--
-- Auto gun pickup (backshot_autogun.lua)
local AutoGunModule = (function()
local AutoGun = {}

local BODY_PARTS = {
    "HumanoidRootPart", "Head", "LeftFoot", "RightFoot",
    "Left Leg", "Right Leg", "UpperTorso", "Torso",
}

function AutoGun.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local Workspace = env.Workspace

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local enabled = false
    local connections = {}
    local chasing = {}
    local highlights = {}

    self.OnMessage = function(text) end

    self.Config = {
        DropName = "GunDrop",
        RetryTime = 3,
        RetryInterval = 0.25,
        ShowHighlight = true,
    }

    local function say(text)
        pcall(self.OnMessage, text)
    end

    local function holdsTool(toolName)
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        return (character and character:FindFirstChild(toolName) ~= nil)
            or (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild(toolName) ~= nil)
    end

    local function dropParts(drop)
        local parts = {}
        if drop:IsA("BasePart") then
            table.insert(parts, drop)
        end
        for _, descendant in drop:GetDescendants() do
            if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                table.insert(parts, descendant)
            end
        end
        return parts
    end

    local function touchDrop(drop)
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not character then return end

        local parts = dropParts(drop)
        for _, bodyName in BODY_PARTS do
            local body = character:FindFirstChild(bodyName)
            if body then
                for _, part in parts do
                    pcall(firetouchinterest, body, part, 0)
                    pcall(firetouchinterest, body, part, 1)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function setHighlight(drop, on)
        local existing = highlights[drop]
        if not on then
            if existing then
                existing:Destroy()
                highlights[drop] = nil
            end
            return
        end
        if existing and existing.Parent then return end

        local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
        highlight.Name = "BackshotGunHighlight"
        highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 60)
        highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        highlight.FillTransparency = 0.4
        highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        highlight.Adornee = drop
        highlight.Parent = drop
        highlights[drop] = highlight
    end

    local function chase(drop)
        if chasing[drop] then return end
        chasing[drop] = true

        task.spawn(function()
            local startedAt = os.clock()
            while enabled and drop.Parent and os.clock() - startedAt < self.Config.RetryTime do
                if holdsTool("Gun") or holdsTool("Knife") then break end
                touchDrop(drop)
                task.wait(self.Config.RetryInterval)
            end
            chasing[drop] = nil
        end)
    end

    local function trackDrop(instance)
        if instance.Name ~= self.Config.DropName then return end

        if self.Config.ShowHighlight then
            setHighlight(instance, true)
        end

        if holdsTool("Knife") or holdsTool("Gun") then return end
        if typeof(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" then
            say("Auto gun: this executor has no firetouchinterest")
            return
        end
        chase(instance)
    end

    -- Watching (the DescendantAdded connection + an initial scan) runs whenever EITHER
    -- auto-pickup or the highlight is wanted, so turning the highlight on by itself (with
    -- auto-pickup off) actually shows drops instead of doing nothing.
    local function refreshWatch()
        if enabled or self.Config.ShowHighlight then
            if not connections.added then
                connections.added = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(trackDrop)
            end
            for _, descendant in Workspace:GetDescendants() do
                if descendant.Name == self.Config.DropName then
                    trackDrop(descendant)
                end
            end
        else
            if connections.added then
                connections.added:Disconnect()
                connections.added = nil
            end
        end
    end

    function self.SetEnabled(state)
        enabled = state and true or false
        refreshWatch()
        if not enabled then
            -- Auto-pickup turned off: drop any in-progress chases, but keep highlights
            -- if the highlight toggle is still on (refreshWatch above decides that).
        end
    end

    function self.SetShowHighlight(state)
        self.Config.ShowHighlight = state and true or false
        refreshWatch()
        if not self.Config.ShowHighlight then
            for drop in pairs(highlights) do
                setHighlight(drop, false)
            end
        end
    end

    -- Turns everything off and removes every highlight. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        enabled, self.Config.ShowHighlight = false, false
        refreshWatch()
        for drop in pairs(highlights) do
            setHighlight(drop, false)
        end
        table.clear(chasing)
    end

    return self
end

return AutoGun
end)()
local AutoGunController = AutoGunModule.new({
    Players = Players,
    Workspace = Workspace,
})
Backshot.AutoGun = AutoGunController
--
-- Fun / novelty visuals (backshot_fun.lua)
local FunModule = (function()
local Fun = {}

function Fun.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local RunService = env.RunService

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local connections = {}
    local buddyModel = nil
    local buddyAnimTrack = nil
    local trail = nil
    local headSaved = nil
    local scaleSaved = nil

    self.Config = {
        BuddyVariant = "Ghost",
        BuddyColor = nil, -- nil = the variant's own colour
        BuddyModelId = nil, -- rbxassetid of a real Model to summon instead of the procedural look
        BuddyAnimationId = nil,      -- only plays if BuddyModelId loads a rigged model
        BuddyAnimationFreeze = false,
        BuddyAnimationTime = 0,
        -- "Become Stand": your own character is tinted to the chosen variant's colour and
        -- material. Full character replacement is not done here -- that would risk breaking
        -- your movement and animations, and I cannot test that reliably in this environment.
        BuddyOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4, -2.5),  -- behind and above you
        BuddyBobSpeed = 1.4,
        BuddyBobHeight = 0.4,
        -- 0 = faces the same way you do, no self-rotation. That spin looked distracting
        -- and made attacking feel random, so it is off by default now.
        BuddySpinSpeed = 0,
        -- "Stand can attack": AttackTargets() -> list of Players in reach of the buddy,
        -- OnAttackTick(player) does the actual hit. The menu wires both.
        AttackEnabled = false,
        AttackRange = 8,
        AttackCooldown = 0.6,
        AttackTargets = nil,
        OnAttackTick = nil,
        HeadScale = 1,
        BodyScale = 1,
        TrailColor = nil, -- nil = rainbow
        LowGravityValue = 40,
    }

    local function disconnect(key)
        local connection = connections[key]
        if connection and connection.Connected then
            connection:Disconnect()
        end
        connections[key] = nil
    end

    local function myCharacter()
        return LocalPlayer.Character
    end

    -- Stand buddy -----------------------------------------------------------

    local VARIANTS = {
        Ghost    = { color = Color3.fromRGB(150, 220, 255), material = Enum.Material.ForceField, transparency = 0.3 },
        Skeleton = { color = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 220), material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, transparency = 0.05 },
        Crystal  = { color = Color3.fromRGB(180, 90, 255), material = Enum.Material.Glass, transparency = 0.15 },
        Shadow   = { color = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 30), material = Enum.Material.Neon, transparency = 0.1 },
    }
    Fun.BuddyVariants = { "Ghost", "Skeleton", "Crystal", "Shadow" }

    local function destroyBuddy()
        disconnect("buddy")
        if buddyAnimTrack then
            pcall(function() buddyAnimTrack:Stop() end)
            buddyAnimTrack = nil
        end
        if buddyModel then
            buddyModel:Destroy()
            buddyModel = nil
        end
    end

    local function buildBuddy()
        local look = VARIANTS[self.Config.BuddyVariant] or VARIANTS.Ghost
        local color = self.Config.BuddyColor or look.color

        local model = Instance.new("Model")
        model.Name = "BackshotStandBuddy"

        local torso = Instance.new("Part")
        torso.Name = "Torso"
        torso.Shape = Enum.PartType.Block
        torso.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2.6, 1)
        torso.Material = look.material
        torso.Color = color
        torso.Anchored = true
        torso.CanCollide = false
        torso.CanQuery = false
        torso.CastShadow = false
        torso.Transparency = look.transparency
        torso.Parent = model

        local function limb(size, offset)
            local part = Instance.new("Part")
            part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Block
            part.Size = size
            part.Material = look.material
            part.Color = color
            part.Anchored = true
            part.CanCollide = false
            part.CanQuery = false
            part.CastShadow = false
            part.Transparency = look.transparency
            part.Parent = model
            return part, offset
        end

        local leftArm, leftArmOffset = limb(Vector3.new(0.6, 2, 0.6), CFrame.new(-1.3, 0, 0))
        local rightArm, rightArmOffset = limb(Vector3.new(0.6, 2, 0.6), CFrame.new(1.3, 0, 0))

        local head = Instance.new("Part")
        head.Name = "Head"
        head.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
        head.Size = Vector3.new(1.4, 1.4, 1.4)
        head.Material = look.material
        head.Color = color
        head.Anchored = true
        head.CanCollide = false
        head.CanQuery = false
        head.CastShadow = false
        head.Transparency = look.transparency
        head.Parent = model

        local glow = Instance.new("PointLight")
        glow.Color = color
        glow.Range = 10
        glow.Brightness = 1.5
        glow.Parent = head

        model.PrimaryPart = torso
        return model, torso, head, leftArm, leftArmOffset, rightArm, rightArmOffset
    end

    local attackCooldowns = {}

    -- Loads a real asset as the buddy model (what you sent IDs for: a model you can summon).
    -- Not verified: the id might not be a Model, might fail to load, or might be huge/tiny.
    -- On any failure this reports why and falls back to the procedural look instead.
    local function loadAssetBuddy(id)
        local okLoad, objects = pcall(function()
            return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id))
        end)
        if not okLoad or type(objects) ~= "table" or not objects[1] then
            return nil, "could not load that asset id"
        end

        local asset = objects[1]
        local model = asset
        if not asset:IsA("Model") then
            -- Some assets come wrapped in a Folder/Tool; pull the first real Model out of it.
            model = asset:IsA("Model") and asset or asset:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Model", true)
            if not model then
                asset:Destroy()
                return nil, "that asset is not a Model"
            end
            model.Parent = nil
        end

        local anyPart = false
        for _, part in model:GetDescendants() do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                anyPart = true
                part.Anchored = true
                part.CanCollide = false
                part.CanQuery = false
                part.CastShadow = false
            end
        end
        if not anyPart then
            model:Destroy()
            return nil, "that model has no parts"
        end

        if not model.PrimaryPart then
            model.PrimaryPart = model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        end
        return model, nil
    end

    -- Plays Config.BuddyAnimationId on the loaded asset, if it has a Humanoid/AnimationController
    -- to play one on. The procedural look has no rig, so it cannot play an animation at all --
    -- animations only work once a real model is summoned via Config.BuddyModelId.
    local function playBuddyAnimation(model)
        if not self.Config.BuddyAnimationId then return end

        local animator = model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Animator", true)
        if not animator then
            local humanoid = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or model:FindFirstChildOfClass("AnimationController")
            if humanoid then
                animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                if not animator then
                    animator = Instance.new("Animator")
                    animator.Parent = humanoid
                end
            end
        end
        if not animator then
            say("Stand buddy: this model has no Humanoid/AnimationController, cannot animate it")
            return
        end

        local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
        animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(self.Config.BuddyAnimationId)
        local okLoad, track = pcall(function()
            return animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
        end)
        if okLoad and track then
            buddyAnimTrack = track
            track.Looped = true
            track:Play()
            if self.Config.BuddyAnimationFreeze then
                track:AdjustSpeed(0)
                track.TimePosition = self.Config.BuddyAnimationTime or 0
            end
        end
    end

    function self.SetStandBuddy(state)
        destroyBuddy()
        if not state then return end

        local model, torso, head, leftArm, leftArmOffset, rightArm, rightArmOffset
        local usingAsset = false

        if self.Config.BuddyModelId then
            local loaded, err = loadAssetBuddy(self.Config.BuddyModelId)
            if loaded then
                model = loaded
                usingAsset = true
            else
                say("Stand buddy: " .. err .. " -- using the procedural look instead")
            end
        end

        if not usingAsset then
            model, torso, head, leftArm, leftArmOffset, rightArm, rightArmOffset = buildBuddy()
        end

        model.Parent = workspace
        buddyModel = model
        table.clear(attackCooldowns)

        if usingAsset then
            playBuddyAnimation(model)
        end

        connections.buddy = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            local character = myCharacter()
            local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not root or not buddyModel then return end

            local t = os.clock()
            local bob = math.sin(t * self.Config.BuddyBobSpeed) * self.Config.BuddyBobHeight
            local spin = (t * self.Config.BuddySpinSpeed) % (2 * math.pi)
            local offset = self.Config.BuddyOffset + Vector3.new(0, bob, 0)
            local cframe = root.CFrame * CFrame.new(offset) * CFrame.Angles(0, spin, 0)

            local attackPosition
            if usingAsset then
                pcall(function() buddyModel:PivotTo(cframe) end)
                attackPosition = cframe.Position
            else
                torso.CFrame = cframe
                head.CFrame = cframe * CFrame.new(0, 1.9, 0)
                leftArm.CFrame = cframe * leftArmOffset
                rightArm.CFrame = cframe * rightArmOffset
                attackPosition = torso.Position
            end

            local okAttack = self.Config.AttackEnabled and typeof(self.Config.OnAttackTick) == "function"
                and typeof(self.Config.AttackTargets) == "function"
            if okAttack then
                local okList, targets = pcall(self.Config.AttackTargets)
                if okList and type(targets) == "table" then
                    for _, target in targets do
                        local targetRoot = target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if targetRoot and (targetRoot.Position - attackPosition).Magnitude <= self.Config.AttackRange then
                            local last = attackCooldowns[target] or -math.huge
                            if t - last >= self.Config.AttackCooldown then
                                attackCooldowns[target] = t
                                pcall(self.Config.OnAttackTick, target)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    function self.IsStandBuddyOn()
        return buddyModel ~= nil
    end

    -- "Become Stand": tints your own character (colour + material) to match a variant,
    -- rather than replacing your whole avatar, which I cannot safely test here.
    local becomeSaved = nil
    local becomeConnection = nil

    function self.SetBecomeStand(state)
        if becomeConnection then
            becomeConnection:Disconnect()
            becomeConnection = nil
        end

        if not state then
            if becomeSaved then
                for part, values in pairs(becomeSaved) do
                    if part.Parent then
                        part.Color = values.Color
                        part.Material = values.Material
                        part.Transparency = values.Transparency
                    end
                end
                becomeSaved = nil
            end
            return
        end

        local VARIANTS_LOOKUP = {
            Ghost    = { color = Color3.fromRGB(150, 220, 255), material = Enum.Material.ForceField, transparency = 0.3 },
            Skeleton = { color = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 220), material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, transparency = 0.05 },
            Crystal  = { color = Color3.fromRGB(180, 90, 255), material = Enum.Material.Glass, transparency = 0.15 },
            Shadow   = { color = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 30), material = Enum.Material.Neon, transparency = 0.1 },
        }
        local look = VARIANTS_LOOKUP[self.Config.BuddyVariant] or VARIANTS_LOOKUP.Ghost

        local function apply(character)
            becomeSaved = becomeSaved or {}
            for _, part in character:GetDescendants() do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if becomeSaved[part] == nil then
                        becomeSaved[part] = { Color = part.Color, Material = part.Material, Transparency = part.Transparency }
                    end
                    part.Color = look.color
                    part.Material = look.material
                    part.Transparency = look.transparency
                end
            end
        end

        local character = myCharacter()
        if character then apply(character) end
        becomeConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newCharacter)
            task.wait(0.5)
            apply(newCharacter)
        end)
    end

    -- Giant / tiny head -------------------------------------------------------

    function self.SetHeadScale(value)
        self.Config.HeadScale = value
        local character = myCharacter()
        local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then return end

        if headSaved == nil then
            headSaved = head.Size
        end
        local mesh = head:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
        if not mesh then
            mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
            mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.Head
            mesh.Parent = head
        end
        mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(value, value, value)
    end

    -- Overall body scale ------------------------------------------------------

    function self.SetBodyScale(value)
        self.Config.BodyScale = value
        local character = myCharacter()
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid then return end

        if scaleSaved == nil then
            scaleSaved = {
                humanoid.BodyHeightScale.Value, humanoid.BodyWidthScale.Value, humanoid.BodyDepthScale.Value,
            }
        end
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.BodyHeightScale.Value = value
            humanoid.BodyWidthScale.Value = value
            humanoid.BodyDepthScale.Value = value
        end)
    end

    function self.ResetScale()
        local character = myCharacter()
        local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        local mesh = head and head:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
        if mesh then mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) end
        headSaved = nil

        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if humanoid and scaleSaved then
            pcall(function()
                humanoid.BodyHeightScale.Value = scaleSaved[1]
                humanoid.BodyWidthScale.Value = scaleSaved[2]
                humanoid.BodyDepthScale.Value = scaleSaved[3]
            end)
        end
        scaleSaved = nil
        self.Config.HeadScale, self.Config.BodyScale = 1, 1
    end

    -- Rainbow trail -----------------------------------------------------------

    local function destroyTrail()
        disconnect("trail")
        if trail then
            trail:Destroy()
            trail = nil
        end
    end

    function self.SetTrail(state)
        destroyTrail()
        if not state then return end

        local character = myCharacter()
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return end

        local attach0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
        attach0.Position = Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0)
        attach0.Parent = root
        local attach1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
        attach1.Position = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
        attach1.Parent = root

        trail = Instance.new("Trail")
        trail.Attachment0 = attach0
        trail.Attachment1 = attach1
        trail.Lifetime = 1
        trail.WidthScale = NumberSequence.new(1, 0)
        trail.Parent = root

        if self.Config.TrailColor then
            trail.Color = ColorSequence.new(self.Config.TrailColor)
        else
            connections.trail = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if trail and trail.Parent then
                    local hue = (os.clock() * 0.2) % 1
                    trail.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromHSV(hue, 1, 1))
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    -- More small fun toggles --------------------------------------------

    local savedGravity = nil
    function self.SetLowGravity(state)
        if state then
            if savedGravity == nil then savedGravity = workspace.Gravity end
            workspace.Gravity = self.Config.LowGravityValue
        elseif savedGravity then
            workspace.Gravity = savedGravity
            savedGravity = nil
        end
    end

    local function attachToRoot(name, build)
        disconnect(name)
        local tag = "BackshotFun_" .. name
        local character = myCharacter()
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local existing = root and root:FindFirstChild(tag)
        if existing then existing:Destroy() end
        if not root then return nil end

        local instance = build()
        instance.Name = tag
        instance.Parent = root
        connections[name] = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newCharacter)
            task.wait(1)
            local newRoot = newCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local already = newRoot and newRoot:FindFirstChild(tag)
            if newRoot and not already then
                local again = build()
                again.Name = tag
                again.Parent = newRoot
            end
        end)
        return instance
    end

    local function removeFromRoot(name)
        disconnect(name)
        local tag = "BackshotFun_" .. name
        local character = myCharacter()
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local existing = root and root:FindFirstChild(tag)
        if existing then existing:Destroy() end
    end

    function self.SetFireAura(state)
        if state then
            attachToRoot("fire", function() return Instance.new("Fire") end)
        else
            removeFromRoot("fire")
        end
    end

    function self.SetSparklesAura(state)
        if state then
            attachToRoot("sparkles", function() return Instance.new("Sparkles") end)
        else
            removeFromRoot("sparkles")
        end
    end

    local discoConnection
    function self.SetDiscoLight(state)
        if discoConnection then
            discoConnection:Disconnect()
            discoConnection = nil
        end
        removeFromRoot("disco")
        if not state then return end

        local light = attachToRoot("disco", function()
            local l = Instance.new("PointLight")
            l.Range = 16
            l.Brightness = 3
            return l
        end)
        discoConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local character = myCharacter()
            local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local current = root and root:FindFirstChild("BackshotFun_disco")
            if current then
                current.Color = Color3.fromHSV((os.clock() * 0.8) % 1, 1, 1)
            end
        end)
        connections.discoLoop = discoConnection
    end

    function self.SetFlashlight(state)
        if state then
            attachToRoot("flashlight", function()
                local l = Instance.new("SpotLight")
                l.Range = 40
                l.Angle = 60
                l.Brightness = 4
                l.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
                return l
            end)
        else
            removeFromRoot("flashlight")
        end
    end

    local rainbowConnection
    local rainbowSaved = {}
    function self.SetRainbowSkin(state)
        if rainbowConnection then
            rainbowConnection:Disconnect()
            rainbowConnection = nil
        end
        if not state then
            for part, color in pairs(rainbowSaved) do
                if part.Parent then part.Color = color end
            end
            table.clear(rainbowSaved)
            return
        end

        rainbowConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local character = myCharacter()
            if not character then return end
            local hue = (os.clock() * 0.25) % 1
            local color = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.8, 1)
            for _, part in character:GetDescendants() do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if rainbowSaved[part] == nil then
                        rainbowSaved[part] = part.Color
                    end
                    part.Color = color
                end
            end
        end)
        connections.rainbow = rainbowConnection
    end

    function self.ScreenShake(strength, seconds)
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end
        strength = strength or 0.4
        local duration = seconds or 0.4
        local startedAt = os.clock()
        local shakeConnection
        shakeConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if os.clock() - startedAt > duration then
                shakeConnection:Disconnect()
                return
            end
            local falloff = 1 - (os.clock() - startedAt) / duration
            local offset = Vector3.new(
                (math.random() - 0.5) * strength * falloff,
                (math.random() - 0.5) * strength * falloff,
                0
            )
            camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(offset)
        end)
    end

    function self.ConfettiBurst()
        local character = myCharacter()
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return end

        local emitter = Instance.new("Attachment")
        emitter.Parent = root
        local particles = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        particles.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
        particles.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.6, 1.2)
        particles.Rate = 0
        particles.Speed = NumberRange.new(8, 16)
        particles.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180)
        particles.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 180), Color3.fromRGB(80, 180, 255))
        particles.Parent = emitter
        particles:Emit(60)
        task.delay(2, function()
            emitter:Destroy()
        end)
    end

    local emojiGui, emojiLabel, emojiIndex = nil, nil, 1
    local EMOJIS = { "😂", "😎", "💀", "🔥", "😡", "❤", "😴" }

    function self.SetEmojiBubble(state)
        disconnect("emoji")
        if emojiGui then
            emojiGui:Destroy()
            emojiGui = nil
        end
        if not state then return end

        local character = myCharacter()
        local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then return end

        emojiGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        emojiGui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 40)
        emojiGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1.3, 0)
        emojiGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
        emojiGui.Adornee = head
        emojiGui.Parent = head

        emojiLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        emojiLabel.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        emojiLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        emojiLabel.TextScaled = true
        emojiLabel.Text = EMOJIS[emojiIndex]
        emojiLabel.Parent = emojiGui

        connections.emoji = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            -- cycles every ~2 seconds; purely decorative
        end)
        task.spawn(function()
            while emojiGui and emojiGui.Parent do
                task.wait(2)
                emojiIndex = emojiIndex % #EMOJIS + 1
                if emojiLabel then emojiLabel.Text = EMOJIS[emojiIndex] end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Turns everything off and restores what was changed. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        destroyBuddy()
        destroyTrail()
        self.SetBecomeStand(false)
        self.ResetScale()
        self.SetLowGravity(false)
        self.SetRainbowSkin(false)
        removeFromRoot("fire")
        removeFromRoot("sparkles")
        removeFromRoot("disco")
        removeFromRoot("flashlight")
        self.SetEmojiBubble(false)
        for key, connection in pairs(connections) do
            if typeof(connection) == "RBXScriptConnection" and connection.Connected then
                connection:Disconnect()
            end
            connections[key] = nil
        end
    end

    return self
end

return Fun
end)()
local FunController = FunModule.new({
    Players = Players,
    RunService = RunService,
})
Backshot.Fun = FunController
--
-- Skin changer (backshot_skins.lua)
local SkinsModule = (function()
local Skins = {}

function Skins.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local enabled = false
    local connections = {}
    local originals = {}      -- [instance] = { property = originalValue, ... }
    local applied = {}        -- [tool] = true

    self.Config = {
        Skins = {},
    }
