    local function track(connection)
        table.insert(connections, connection)
        return connection
    end

    local function remember(object, property)
        originals[object] = originals[object] or {}
        if originals[object][property] == nil then
            local ok, value = pcall(function() return object[property] end)
            if ok then
                originals[object][property] = value
            end
        end
    end

    local function setProperty(object, property, value)
        remember(object, property)
        pcall(function() object[property] = value end)
    end

    local function applySkinToTool(tool, skin)
        for _, part in tool:GetDescendants() do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                if skin.Color then
                    setProperty(part, "Color", skin.Color)
                end
                if skin.TextureId and part:IsA("MeshPart") then
                    setProperty(part, "TextureID", skin.TextureId)
                end
                if skin.MeshId and part:IsA("MeshPart") then
                    setProperty(part, "MeshId", skin.MeshId)
                end
            elseif part:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
                if skin.TextureId then
                    setProperty(part, "TextureId", skin.TextureId)
                end
                if skin.MeshId then
                    setProperty(part, "MeshId", skin.MeshId)
                end
            end
        end
        applied[tool] = true
    end

    local function restoreTool(tool)
        for _, part in tool:GetDescendants() do
            local saved = originals[part]
            if saved then
                for property, value in pairs(saved) do
                    pcall(function() part[property] = value end)
                end
                originals[part] = nil
            end
        end
        applied[tool] = nil
    end

    local function handleTool(instance)
        if not enabled then return end
        if not instance:IsA("Tool") then return end

        local skin = self.Config.Skins[instance.Name]
        if skin then
            applySkinToTool(instance, skin)
        end
    end

    local function scan(container)
        for _, child in container:GetChildren() do
            handleTool(child)
        end
    end

    local function watch(container)
        if not container then return end
        track(container.ChildAdded:Connect(handleTool))
        scan(container)
    end

    -- Re-applies Config.Skins to whatever tools you currently have: reverts every tool
    -- this module changed, then re-scans your Character and Backpack. Call this after
    -- changing Config.Skins while already enabled (picking a skin does not retroactively
    -- touch a tool you are already holding otherwise, since nothing re-fires for it).
    function self.Reapply()
        for tool in pairs(applied) do
            restoreTool(tool)
        end
        if not enabled then return end
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        if character then scan(character) end
        if backpack then scan(backpack) end
    end

    function self.SetEnabled(state)
        enabled = state and true or false

        if enabled then
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            watch(character)
            watch(backpack)
        else
            for tool in pairs(applied) do
                restoreTool(tool)
            end
        end
    end

    -- Restores every changed part and disconnects everything. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        enabled = false
        for tool in pairs(applied) do
            restoreTool(tool)
        end
        for _, connection in connections do
            if connection.Connected then
                connection:Disconnect()
            end
        end
        table.clear(connections)
    end

    -- Re-apply when the character respawns, since it is a new set of tools.
    track(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
        if not enabled then return end
        task.wait(0.5)
        watch(character)
    end))

    return self
end

return Skins
end)()
local SkinsController = SkinsModule.new({ Players = Players })
Backshot.Skins = SkinsController
-- Skin catalog: data only, taken from the user's own exported skin list.
local SkinsCatalog = (function()
local Catalog = {

    {name = "Fang", kind = "Knife", rarity = "Godly", event = nil, meshId = 117500241, textureId = 117500388, colour = {99, 95, 98}},
    {name = "Icepiercer", kind = "Gun", rarity = "Ancient", event = "Christmas", meshId = nil, textureId = nil, colour = {163, 162, 165}},

-- Pure helpers, kept free of Roblox objects so they can be checked on their own.

-- True for a knife sound whose name looks like a hit/stab/swing sound.
local function hitName(name)
    local lower = string.lower(name)
    return string.find(lower, "stab", 1, true) ~= nil
        or string.find(lower, "slash", 1, true) ~= nil
        or string.find(lower, "swing", 1, true) ~= nil
        or string.find(lower, "hit", 1, true) ~= nil
        or string.find(lower, "kill", 1, true) ~= nil
        or string.find(lower, "knife", 1, true) ~= nil
end

-- True if a death at `now` counts as your kill, given your last attack time.
local function countsAsMyKill(now, lastAttack, window)
    return (now - lastAttack) <= window
end

function Sounds.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local ReplicatedStorage = env.ReplicatedStorage
    local SoundService = env.SoundService

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local connections = {}
    local toolConnections = {}
    local deathConnections = {}
    local hooked = {}
    local muted = {}
    local enabled = false
    local lastAttack = -math.huge
    local lastRoundEnd = -math.huge

    self.Config = {
        Volume = 0.8,
        MuteOriginal = false,
        KillWindow = 2,
        Events = {
            Shoot = { Enabled = false, Sound = "RPGS Candy" },
            Stab = { Enabled = false, Sound = "Bonk" },
            Kill = { Enabled = false, Sound = "Golden Knife Kill" },
            RoundStart = { Enabled = false, Sound = "Bell" },
            RoundEnd = { Enabled = false, Sound = "Fatality" },
        },
    }

    local function playSound(soundName)
        local id = PACKS[soundName]
        if not id then return false end

        local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
        sound.Volume = math.clamp(self.Config.Volume, 0, 5)
        sound.Parent = SoundService
        sound.Ended:Connect(function()
            sound:Destroy()
        end)
        sound:Play()
        task.delay(10, function()
            if sound.Parent then sound:Destroy() end
        end)
        return true
    end

    local function play(eventName)
        local event = self.Config.Events[eventName]
        if event and event.Enabled then
            playSound(event.Sound)
        end
    end

    -- Plays an event's sound once even if the event is switched off, so you can try it.
    function self.Test(eventName)
        local event = self.Config.Events[eventName]
        if event then
            return playSound(event.Sound)
        end
        return false
    end

    function self.SetEventEnabled(eventName, state)
        local event = self.Config.Events[eventName]
        if event then
            event.Enabled = state and true or false
        end
    end

    function self.SetEventSound(eventName, soundName)
        local event = self.Config.Events[eventName]
        if event and PACKS[soundName] then
            event.Sound = soundName
        end
    end

    function self.SetVolume(value)
        self.Config.Volume = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 0.8, 0, 5)
    end

    -- Muting the tool's own sounds -----------------------------------------

    local function toolSounds(tool, isGun)
        local found = {}
        local soundsFolder = tool:FindFirstChild("Sounds")
        local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if isGun then
            local shoot = soundsFolder and soundsFolder:FindFirstChild("Shoot")
            if shoot and shoot:IsA("Sound") then table.insert(found, shoot) end
            local gunshot = handle and handle:FindFirstChild("Gunshot")
            if gunshot and gunshot:IsA("Sound") then table.insert(found, gunshot) end
        else
            if soundsFolder then
                for _, child in soundsFolder:GetChildren() do
                    if child:IsA("Sound") then table.insert(found, child) end
                end
            end
            if handle then
                for _, child in handle:GetChildren() do
                    if child:IsA("Sound") and hitName(child.Name) then table.insert(found, child) end
                end
            end
        end
        return found
    end

    local function muteTool(tool)
        for _, sound in toolSounds(tool, tool.Name == "Gun") do
            if muted[sound] == nil then
                muted[sound] = sound.Volume
            end
            sound.Volume = 0
        end
    end

    local function restoreMuted()
        for sound, volume in pairs(muted) do
            if sound.Parent then
                sound.Volume = volume
            end
        end
        table.clear(muted)
    end

    function self.SetMuteOriginal(state)
        self.Config.MuteOriginal = state and true or false
        if self.Config.MuteOriginal then
            for tool in pairs(toolConnections) do
                if tool.Parent then muteTool(tool) end
            end
        else
            restoreMuted()
        end
    end

    -- Tool and death tracking ---------------------------------------------

    local function watchTool(instance)
        if not instance:IsA("Tool") then return end
        if instance.Name ~= "Gun" and instance.Name ~= "Knife" then return end
        if toolConnections[instance] then return end

        local isGun = instance.Name == "Gun"
        toolConnections[instance] = instance.Activated:Connect(function()
            lastAttack = os.clock()
            play(isGun and "Shoot" or "Stab")
        end)
        if self.Config.MuteOriginal then
            muteTool(instance)
        end
    end

    local function watchContainer(container)
        if not container then return end
        for _, child in container:GetChildren() do
            watchTool(child)
        end
        table.insert(connections, container.ChildAdded:Connect(watchTool))
    end

    local function watchPlayerDeaths(player)
        if player == LocalPlayer then return end

        local function hook(character)
            local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
            if not humanoid then return end
            if deathConnections[player] then
                deathConnections[player]:Disconnect()
            end
            deathConnections[player] = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
                if countsAsMyKill(os.clock(), lastAttack, self.Config.KillWindow) then
                    play("Kill")
                end
            end)
        end

        if player.Character then
            task.spawn(hook, player.Character)
        end
        table.insert(connections, player.CharacterAdded:Connect(hook))
    end

    -- Round events ---------------------------------------------------------

    local function hookRemote(remote)
        if hooked[remote] or not remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then return end

        if remote.Name == "RoundStart" then
            hooked[remote] = true
            table.insert(connections, remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
                play("RoundStart")
            end))
        elseif remote.Name == "RoundEndFade" or remote.Name == "VictoryScreen" then
            hooked[remote] = true
            table.insert(connections, remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - lastRoundEnd > 3 then
                    lastRoundEnd = now
                    play("RoundEnd")
                end
            end))
        end
    end

    function self.SetEnabled(state)
        if state == enabled then return end
        enabled = state and true or false

        if not enabled then
            for _, connection in connections do
                if connection.Connected then connection:Disconnect() end
            end
            table.clear(connections)
            for _, connection in pairs(toolConnections) do
                connection:Disconnect()
            end
            table.clear(toolConnections)
            for _, connection in pairs(deathConnections) do
                connection:Disconnect()
            end
            table.clear(deathConnections)
            table.clear(hooked)
            restoreMuted()
            return
        end

        -- Your tools: in the Character and in the Backpack, now and after respawns.
        watchContainer(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
        if LocalPlayer.Character then
            watchContainer(LocalPlayer.Character)
        end
        table.insert(connections, LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
            watchContainer(character)
            watchContainer(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
        end))

        for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
            watchPlayerDeaths(player)
        end
        table.insert(connections, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(watchPlayerDeaths))

        for _, descendant in ReplicatedStorage:GetDescendants() do
            pcall(hookRemote, descendant)
        end
        table.insert(connections, ReplicatedStorage.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
            pcall(hookRemote, descendant)
        end))
    end

    -- Turns everything off, restores the tool sounds. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        self.SetEnabled(false)
    end

    -- Exposed for isolated checks.
    self._internal = { hitName = hitName, countsAsMyKill = countsAsMyKill }

    return self
end

return Sounds
end)()
local SoundsController = SoundsModule.new({
    Players = Players,
    ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"),
    SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService"),
})
Backshot.Sounds = SoundsController
--
-- Utilities (backshot_tools.lua)
local ToolsModule = (function()
local Tools = {}

local function isEffect(instance)
    return instance:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or instance:IsA("Trail") or instance:IsA("Beam")
        or instance:IsA("Smoke") or instance:IsA("Fire") or instance:IsA("Sparkles")
end

function Tools.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local RunService = env.RunService
    local Workspace = env.Workspace
    local UserInputService = env.UserInputService
    local TeleportService = env.TeleportService
    local Lighting = env.Lighting
    local HttpService = env.HttpService

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local connections = {}
    local lastSafe = nil
    local savedZoom = nil
    local boostSaved = nil
    local flinging = false
    local antiChanged = {}
    local ringPart = nil
    local dimmed = false

    self.OnMessage = function(text) end

    self.Config = {
        SpinSpeed = 20,
        FlingSpin = 80000,          -- Zenith default
        FlingSeconds = 2.2,         -- Zenith default
        FlingCooldown = 6,          -- Zenith default, used by the fling aura
        FlingAuraRange = 15,
        FlingFilter = function(player)
            return player ~= LocalPlayer
        end,
        AuraRadius = 20,
        AntiFlingMaxSpeed = 150,
        AntiFlingMaxSpin = 50,
        InvisDirection = "Up",      -- "Up" or "Down"
        InvisDepth = 200000,
    }

    local function say(text)
        pcall(self.OnMessage, text)
    end

    local function disconnect(key)
        local connection = connections[key]
        if connection and connection.Connected then
            connection:Disconnect()
        end
        connections[key] = nil
    end

    local function myRoot()
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if root and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
            return root, humanoid
        end
        return nil, nil
    end

    function self.SetAntiAfk(state)
        disconnect("afk")
        if not state then return end
        connections.afk = LocalPlayer.Idled:Connect(function()
            pcall(function()
                local virtualUser = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
                virtualUser:CaptureController()
                virtualUser:ClickButton2(Vector2.new(0, 0))
            end)
        end)
    end

    function self.SetClickTp(state)
        disconnect("clickTp")
        if not state then return end
        connections.clickTp = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
            if processed then return end
            local kind = input.UserInputType
            if kind ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and kind ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end

            local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            local root = myRoot()
            if not camera or not root then return end

            local ray = camera:ScreenPointToRay(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
            local params = RaycastParams.new()
            params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LocalPlayer.Character }
            local result = Workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 2000, params)
            if result then
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(result.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
            end
        end)
    end

    local spinSavedAutoRotate = nil
    local spinning = false

    -- Roblox's own Humanoid.AutoRotate constantly turns you to face your move direction,
    -- which fights a raw AssemblyAngularVelocity spin and can make it look like nothing
    -- happens. AutoRotate is turned off while spinning and restored afterwards. `spinning`
    -- is also checked by Anti Fling's self-clamp, so your OWN spin above its spin threshold
    -- is never mistaken for a fling and zeroed out.
    function self.SetSpin(state)
        disconnect("spin")
        local _, humanoid = myRoot()
        spinning = state and true or false

        if state then
            if humanoid and spinSavedAutoRotate == nil then
                spinSavedAutoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate
                humanoid.AutoRotate = false
            end
            connections.spin = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local root, currentHumanoid = myRoot()
                if root then
                    if currentHumanoid and currentHumanoid.AutoRotate then
                        currentHumanoid.AutoRotate = false
                    end
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, self.Config.SpinSpeed, 0)
                end
            end)
        else
            local root = myRoot()
            if root then
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end
            if humanoid and spinSavedAutoRotate ~= nil then
                humanoid.AutoRotate = spinSavedAutoRotate
            end
            spinSavedAutoRotate = nil
        end
    end

    function self.SetAntiVoid(state)
        disconnect("void")
        lastSafe = nil
        if not state then return end
        connections.void = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local root, humanoid = myRoot()
            if not root then return end

            local limit = Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight + 60
            if humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air and root.Position.Y > limit + 50 then
                lastSafe = root.CFrame
            elseif root.Position.Y < limit and lastSafe then
                root.CFrame = lastSafe
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end
        end)
    end

    function self.SetFpsCap(value)
        if typeof(setfpscap) ~= "function" then
            say("FPS cap: this executor has no setfpscap")
            return false
        end
        pcall(setfpscap, value)
        return true
    end

    function self.SetZoomUnlock(state)
        if state then
            if not savedZoom then
                savedZoom = { LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance, LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance }
            end
            LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 1000
            LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0.5
        elseif savedZoom then
            LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = savedZoom[1]
            LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = savedZoom[2]
            savedZoom = nil
        end
    end

    function self.SetFpsBoost(state)
        disconnect("boost")
        if state then
            if boostSaved then return end
            boostSaved = { shadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows, disabled = {} }
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = false

            local function handle(instance)
                if isEffect(instance) and instance.Enabled then
                    boostSaved.disabled[instance] = true
                    instance.Enabled = false
                end
            end
            for _, descendant in Workspace:GetDescendants() do
                handle(descendant)
            end
            connections.boost = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(handle)
        elseif boostSaved then
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = boostSaved.shadows
            for instance in pairs(boostSaved.disabled) do
                if instance.Parent then
                    instance.Enabled = true
                end
            end
            boostSaved = nil
        end
    end

    function self.Rejoin()
        say("Rejoining this server...")
        local ok = pcall(function()
            TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, LocalPlayer)
        end)
        if not ok then
            say("Rejoin failed")
        end
    end

    function self.ServerHop()
        task.spawn(function()
            local url = string.format(
                "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100",
                game.PlaceId
            )
            local okGet, body = pcall(function()
                return game:HttpGet(url)
            end)
            if not okGet then
                say("Server hop: HttpGet is not available in this executor")
                return
            end

            local okDecode, data = pcall(function()
                return HttpService:JSONDecode(body)
            end)
            if not okDecode or type(data) ~= "table" or type(data.data) ~= "table" then
                say("Server hop: unexpected answer from the server list")
                return
            end

            local candidates = {}
            for _, server in data.data do
                if type(server) == "table" and server.id ~= game.JobId
                    and type(server.playing) == "number" and type(server.maxPlayers) == "number"
                    and server.playing < server.maxPlayers - 1 then
                    table.insert(candidates, server.id)
                end
            end
            if #candidates == 0 then
                say("Server hop: no free server found")
                return
            end

            local pick = candidates[math.random(1, #candidates)]
            say("Server hop: moving...")
            pcall(function()
                TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, pick, LocalPlayer)
            end)
        end)
    end

    -- Runs the fling on each player in `targets`, one after another, then puts you back.
    local function runFling(targets, seconds)
        local root, humanoid = myRoot()
        if not root or not humanoid then
            say("Fling: character not ready")
            return false
        end

        flinging = true
        local saved = root.CFrame
        local startingHealth = humanoid.Health
        task.spawn(function()
            for _, target in targets do
                local startedAt = os.clock()
                while flinging and os.clock() - startedAt < seconds do
                    local currentRoot, currentHumanoid = myRoot()
                    local targetRoot = target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not currentRoot or not targetRoot then break end
                    -- Safety abort: if something at the target's spot is hurting you
                    -- (lava, a kill brick, fall damage), stop immediately instead of
                    -- holding you there for the rest of the fling's duration.
                    if currentHumanoid and currentHumanoid.Health < startingHealth then
                        break
                    end
                    currentRoot.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame
                    currentRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, self.Config.FlingSpin, 0)
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                end
                if not flinging then break end
            end

            local finalRoot = myRoot()
            if finalRoot then
                finalRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                finalRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                finalRoot.CFrame = saved
            end
            flinging = false
        end)
        return true
    end

    function self.FlingPlayer(target, seconds)
        if flinging then return false end
        local targetRoot = target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not targetRoot then
            say("Fling: pick a player first")
            return false
        end
        return runFling({ target }, seconds or self.Config.FlingSeconds)
    end

    -- Stops a fling early and puts you back where you were, in case it got stuck.
    function self.CancelFling()
        flinging = false
    end

    function self.FlingPlayers(list, seconds)
        if flinging then return false end
        local targets = {}
        for _, player in list do
            if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                table.insert(targets, player)
            end
        end
        if #targets == 0 then
            say("Fling: nobody to fling")
            return false
        end
        return runFling(targets, seconds or self.Config.FlingSeconds)
    end

    -- Fling aura: when somebody comes within FlingAuraRange, flings the nearest one, then waits FlingCooldown.
    function self.SetFlingAura(state)
        disconnect("flingAura")
        if not state then return end

        local lastFling = -math.huge
        connections.flingAura = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if flinging then return end
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - lastFling < self.Config.FlingCooldown then return end

            local root = myRoot()
            if not root then return end

            local best, bestDistance = nil, self.Config.FlingAuraRange
            for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
                if player ~= LocalPlayer then
                    local okFilter, allowed = pcall(self.Config.FlingFilter, player)
                    local targetRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if okFilter and allowed and targetRoot then
                        local distance = (targetRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                        if distance <= bestDistance then
                            best, bestDistance = player, distance
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if best then
                lastFling = now
                self.FlingPlayer(best, self.Config.FlingSeconds)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function restoreCollisions()
        for part in pairs(antiChanged) do
            if part.Parent then
                part.CanCollide = true
            end
        end
        table.clear(antiChanged)
    end

    -- Anti fling (Zenith's technique, rewritten), with a known limit: it stops the common
    -- client-side push (another player's body colliding into yours, which this disables),
    -- and zeroes your own velocity the instant it looks like a fling. It cannot stop a hit
    -- that the SERVER itself moves you with (an explosion or force remote the game accepts),
    -- because the server is the authority on your position and this only runs on your client.
    -- That is why you can still occasionally get flung even with this on.
    local function clampSelf()
        if flinging then return end
        local root = myRoot()
        if not root then return end

        local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local flat = Vector3.new(velocity.X, math.max(velocity.Y, 0), velocity.Z)
        if flat.Magnitude > self.Config.AntiFlingMaxSpeed then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end
        -- Skipped while Spin is on: that is your own angular velocity, not a fling.
        if not spinning and root.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude > self.Config.AntiFlingMaxSpin then
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end
    end

    function self.SetAntiFling(state)
        disconnect("antiFling")
        disconnect("antiFlingHb")
        restoreCollisions()
        if not state then return end

        connections.antiFling = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
            for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
                if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Character then
                    for _, part in player.Character:GetChildren() do
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide then
                            antiChanged[part] = true
                            part.CanCollide = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            clampSelf()
        end)
        -- Second pass, so a push between two Stepped frames is still caught quickly.
        connections.antiFlingHb = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(clampSelf)
    end

    local function dimCharacter(on)
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not character then return end
        for _, part in character:GetDescendants() do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.LocalTransparencyModifier = on and 0.5 or 0
            end
        end
    end

    -- Invisible (Candy's technique, rewritten): each frame, right after physics, your root part
    -- is moved far away while the game replicates it, then put back before your screen is drawn.
    -- The humanoid's CameraOffset keeps your camera where you are. You look half see-through to yourself.
    function self.SetInvisible(state)
        disconnect("invis")
        disconnect("invisChar")

        if not state then
            local _, humanoid = myRoot()
            if humanoid then
                humanoid.CameraOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end
            if dimmed then
                dimCharacter(false)
                dimmed = false
            end
            return
        end

        dimCharacter(true)
        dimmed = true
        connections.invisChar = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            task.wait(0.5)
            if dimmed then dimCharacter(true) end
        end)

        connections.invis = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local root, humanoid = myRoot()
            if not root then return end

            local saved = root.CFrame
            local savedOffset = humanoid.CameraOffset
            local sign = self.Config.InvisDirection == "Down" and -1 or 1
            local hidden = saved * CFrame.new(0, sign * self.Config.InvisDepth, 0)

            root.CFrame = hidden
            humanoid.CameraOffset = hidden:ToObjectSpace(saved).Position
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
            if root.Parent then
                root.CFrame = saved
            end
            humanoid.CameraOffset = savedOffset
        end)
    end

    -- A flat ring on the floor under you with the aura radius, only visible on your screen.
    function self.SetAuraRing(state)
        disconnect("ring")
        if ringPart then
            ringPart:Destroy()
            ringPart = nil
        end
        if not state then return end

        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end

        ringPart = Instance.new("Part")
        ringPart.Name = "BackshotAuraRing"
        ringPart.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
        ringPart.Anchored = true
        ringPart.CanCollide = false
        ringPart.CanQuery = false
        ringPart.CanTouch = false
        ringPart.CastShadow = false
        ringPart.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        ringPart.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
        ringPart.Transparency = 1
        ringPart.Parent = camera

        connections.ring = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not ringPart then return end
            local root = myRoot()
            if not root then
                ringPart.Transparency = 1
                return
            end
            local diameter = self.Config.AuraRadius * 2
            ringPart.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, diameter, diameter)
            ringPart.Transparency = 0.65
            ringPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position - Vector3.new(0, 2.9, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2)
        end)
    end

    -- Turns everything off and restores what was changed. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        flinging = false
        self.SetAntiAfk(false)
        self.SetClickTp(false)
        self.SetSpin(false)
        self.SetAntiVoid(false)
        self.SetZoomUnlock(false)
        self.SetFpsBoost(false)
        self.SetFlingAura(false)
        self.SetAntiFling(false)
        self.SetInvisible(false)
        self.SetAuraRing(false)
        self.SetSpin(false)
    end

    return self
end

return Tools
end)()
local ToolsController = ToolsModule.new({
    Players = Players,
    RunService = RunService,
    Workspace = Workspace,
    UserInputService = UserInputService,
    TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService"),
    Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting"),
    HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService"),
})
Backshot.Tools = ToolsController
--
-- Gun auto shoot (backshot_gunshot.lua)
local GunShotModule = (function()
local GunShot = {}

-- Pure helper, kept free of Roblox objects so it can be checked on its own.
local function offCooldown(now, lastTime, cooldown)
    return (now - lastTime) >= cooldown
end

function GunShot.new(env)
    local Players = env.Players
    local RunService = env.RunService
    local Workspace = env.Workspace

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local self = {}
    local lastShot = -math.huge
    local lastWarn = -math.huge
    local busy = false
    local autoConnection

    self.OnMessage = function(text) end

    self.Config = {
        -- function() -> Player or nil. Set by the menu (the murderer).
        GetTarget = nil,
        Cooldown = 1.5,
        AimPart = "HumanoidRootPart",
        Prediction = 0,
        CheckWalls = false,  -- off by default: the server resolves the remote, a wall on your screen does not stop it
        AutoEquip = true,
        SilentGunEnabled = false,
    }

    local function say(text)
        pcall(self.OnMessage, text)
    end

    local function warnOnce(text)
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastWarn >= 5 then
            lastWarn = now
            say(text)
        end
    end

    local function findGun()
        local containers = { LocalPlayer.Character, LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack") }
        for _, container in containers do
            if container then
                for _, tool in container:GetChildren() do
                    if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                        local remote = tool:FindFirstChild("Shoot")
                        if remote and (remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") or remote:IsA("RemoteFunction")) then
                            return tool, remote
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return nil, nil
    end

    -- Fires one shot at `player`. Returns true if a shot was sent.
    function self.ShootAt(player)
        if busy then return false end

        local now = os.clock()
        if not offCooldown(now, lastShot, self.Config.Cooldown) then return false end

        local gun, remote = findGun()
        if not gun then
            warnOnce("Gun: you have no gun")
            return false
        end

        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local targetCharacter = player and player.Character
        local targetPart = targetCharacter and targetCharacter:FindFirstChild(self.Config.AimPart)
        local targetHumanoid = targetCharacter and targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not (character and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0) then return false end
        if not (targetPart and targetHumanoid and targetHumanoid.Health > 0) then return false end

        local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not head then return false end
        local origin = head.Position
        local aimPoint = targetPart.Position + targetPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity * self.Config.Prediction

        if self.Config.CheckWalls then
            local params = RaycastParams.new()
            params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { character, targetCharacter }
            if Workspace:Raycast(origin, aimPoint - origin, params) then
                return false
            end
        end

        busy = true
        lastShot = now
        task.spawn(function()
            if gun.Parent ~= character and self.Config.AutoEquip then
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid:EquipTool(gun)
                end)
                task.wait(0.1)
            end

            -- CFrame.lookAt breaks down (can return a NaN rotation) when the look direction is
            -- almost exactly vertical, which happens a lot on MM2's stairs and ladders. Nudge the
            -- up-vector in that case so the shot still has a valid rotation, which is likely why
            -- shots on a standing target sometimes silently failed.
            local direction = aimPoint - origin
            local aim
            if direction.Magnitude < 0.01 then
                aim = CFrame.new(origin)
            elseif math.abs(direction.Unit:Dot(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))) > 0.999 then
                aim = CFrame.lookAt(origin, aimPoint, Vector3.new(0, 0, 1))
            else
                aim = CFrame.lookAt(origin, aimPoint)
            end
            local point = CFrame.new(aimPoint)
            pcall(function()
                if remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                    remote:FireServer(aim, point)
                else
                    remote:InvokeServer(aim, point)
                end
            end)
            busy = false
        end)
        return true
    end

    function self.ShootOnce()
        local getTarget = self.Config.GetTarget
        local target = typeof(getTarget) == "function" and getTarget() or nil
        if not target then
            warnOnce("Gun: no target found yet")
            return false
        end
        return self.ShootAt(target)
    end

    -- Fires on your own click: hooks the Gun tool's Activated event (your actual tap on
    -- the fire button), and on every real shot, also fires a redirected shot at the target.
    -- You can look anywhere; the redirected shot always aims at the target's position.
    local toolConnections = {}

    local function watchGunClicks(instance)
        if not instance:IsA("Tool") or instance.Name ~= "Gun" then return end
        if toolConnections[instance] then return end
        toolConnections[instance] = instance.Activated:Connect(function()
            if self.Config.SilentGunEnabled then
                self.ShootOnce()
            end
        end)
    end

    local function watchContainer(container)
        if not container then return end
        for _, child in container:GetChildren() do
            watchGunClicks(child)
        end
        table.insert(self._silentConnections, container.ChildAdded:Connect(watchGunClicks))
    end

    self._silentConnections = {}
    local silentSetup = false

    function self.SetSilentGun(state)
        self.Config.SilentGunEnabled = state and true or false
        if self.Config.SilentGunEnabled and not silentSetup then
            silentSetup = true
            watchContainer(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
            if LocalPlayer.Character then watchContainer(LocalPlayer.Character) end
            table.insert(self._silentConnections, LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
                watchContainer(character)
                watchContainer(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
            end))
        end
    end

    function self.SetAuto(state)
        if autoConnection then
            autoConnection:Disconnect()
            autoConnection = nil
        end
        if state then
            autoConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                self.ShootOnce()
            end)
        end
    end

    -- Stops auto-shooting and the click hook. Safe to call more than once.
    function self.Unload()
        self.SetAuto(false)
        self.Config.SilentGunEnabled = false
        for tool, connection in pairs(toolConnections) do
            connection:Disconnect()
        end
        table.clear(toolConnections)
        for _, connection in self._silentConnections do
            if connection.Connected then connection:Disconnect() end
        end
        table.clear(self._silentConnections)
        silentSetup = false
    end

    -- Exposed for isolated checks.
    self._internal = { offCooldown = offCooldown }

    return self
end

return GunShot
end)()
local GunShotController = GunShotModule.new({
    Players = Players,
    RunService = RunService,
    Workspace = Workspace,
})
Backshot.GunShot = GunShotController
--
--
-- Helpers the UI library does not provide: notifications and a text prompt.
local function GuiParent()
    local ok, container = pcall(function()
        return gethui()
    end)
    if ok and container then return container end
    return Client:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
end

local NotifyGui, NotifyLabel
local NotifyToken = 0
local function Notify(text)
    local parent = GuiParent()
    if not parent then return end
    if not NotifyGui or not NotifyGui.Parent then
        NotifyGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        NotifyGui.Name = "BackshotNotify"
        NotifyGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        NotifyGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        NotifyGui.DisplayOrder = 20000
        NotifyGui.Parent = parent

        NotifyLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        NotifyLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
        NotifyLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 12)
        NotifyLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(380, 34)
        NotifyLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 22, 30)
        NotifyLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
        NotifyLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
        NotifyLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        NotifyLabel.TextSize = 14
        NotifyLabel.TextWrapped = true
        NotifyLabel.Visible = false
        NotifyLabel.Parent = NotifyGui
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        corner.Parent = NotifyLabel
    end
    NotifyToken += 1
    local token = NotifyToken
    NotifyLabel.Text = tostring(text)
    NotifyLabel.Visible = true
    task.delay(3, function()
        if token == NotifyToken and NotifyLabel then
            NotifyLabel.Visible = false
        end
    end)
end

local PromptGui
local function PromptText(title, default, onSubmit, onClose)
    if PromptGui then
        PromptGui:Destroy()
        PromptGui = nil
    end
    local parent = GuiParent()
    if not parent then
        if onClose then onClose() end
        return
    end

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "BackshotPrompt"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.DisplayOrder = 20001
    gui.Parent = parent
    PromptGui = gui

    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.35, 0)
    frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 124)
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 22, 30)
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Parent = gui

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = tostring(title)
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.TextSize = 14
    label.Parent = frame

    local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
    box.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 34)
    box.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 32)
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 38, 50)
    box.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
    box.PlaceholderText = "type here"
    box.Text = tostring(default or "")
    box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    box.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    box.TextSize = 14
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.Parent = frame

    local function close()
        if gui.Parent then gui:Destroy() end
        if PromptGui == gui then PromptGui = nil end
        if onClose then onClose() end
    end

    local function makeButton(text, x, action)
        local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
        button.Position = UDim2.new(x, x == 0 and 10 or -10, 0, 76)
        button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(x, 0)
        button.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -15, 0, 32)
        button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(78, 139, 255)
        button.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        button.Text = text
        button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        button.TextSize = 14
        button.BorderSizePixel = 0
        button.Parent = frame
        button.Activated:Connect(action)
    end
    makeButton("OK", 0, function()
        pcall(onSubmit, box.Text)
        close()
    end)
    makeButton("Cancel", 1, close)
end

local function DestroyUi()
    local containers = {}
    pcall(function() table.insert(containers, gethui()) end)
    pcall(function() table.insert(containers, game:GetService("CoreGui")) end)
    pcall(function() table.insert(containers, Client:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")) end)
    for _, container in containers do
        for _, name in { "NeverloseCS2", "BackshotNotify", "BackshotPrompt", "BackshotToggle", "BackshotPicker" } do
            local gui = container and container:FindFirstChild(name)
            if gui then gui:Destroy() end
        end
    end
end

local function UnloadAll()
    for _, controller in pairs(Backshot) do
        pcall(function() controller.Unload() end)
    end
    DestroyUi()
end

-- Floating on/off button: tap shows or hides the menu, drag moves the button.
local function BuildToggleButton(window)
    local parent = GuiParent()
    if not parent then return end

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "BackshotToggle"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.DisplayOrder = 20003
    gui.Parent = parent

    local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    button.Name = "Toggle"
    button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    button.Position = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0.88, 0)
    button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 46)
    button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 22, 30)
    button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
    button.Text = "B"
    button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    button.TextSize = 22
    button.AutoButtonColor = false
    button.BorderSizePixel = 0
    button.Parent = gui

    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    corner.Parent = button
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Thickness = 2
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(78, 139, 255)
    stroke.Parent = button

    local function refreshLook()
        local ok, open = pcall(window.IsMenuOpen)
        stroke.Color = (ok and open) and Color3.fromRGB(78, 139, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 120)
    end

    local connections = {}
    local dragStart, startCenter, moved = nil, nil, false

    local function isPointer(input)
        local kind = input.UserInputType
        return kind == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or kind == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
    end

    table.insert(connections, button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if not isPointer(input) then return end
        dragStart = input.Position
        startCenter = button.AbsolutePosition + button.AbsoluteSize / 2
        moved = false
    end))

    table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragStart then return end
        local kind = input.UserInputType
        if kind ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and kind ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end

        local delta = Vector2.new(input.Position.X - dragStart.X, input.Position.Y - dragStart.Y)
        if not moved and delta.Magnitude < 8 then return end
        moved = true

        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
        local x = math.clamp(startCenter.X + delta.X, 23, viewport.X - 23)
        local y = math.clamp(startCenter.Y + delta.Y, 23, viewport.Y - 23)
        button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
    end))

    table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragStart or not isPointer(input) then return end
        local wasTap = not moved
        dragStart = nil
        if wasTap then
            pcall(window.ToggleMenu)
            refreshLook()
        end
    end))

    gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
        for _, connection in connections do
            connection:Disconnect()
        end
    end)

    refreshLook()
end

BuilderController.OnMessage = Notify
SilentAimController.OnMessage = Notify
AutoGunController.OnMessage = Notify
ToolsController.OnMessage = Notify
GunShotController.OnMessage = Notify
GunShotController.Config.GetTarget = function()
    return RolesController.FindByRole("Murderer")
end

-- Who the aim assist may target. Matches the round: the sheriff aims at the murderer.
local AimTargetMode = "Everyone"
AimController.Config.Filter = function(player)
    if player == Client then return false end
    if AimTargetMode == "Murderer" then
        return RolesController.GetRole(player) == "Murderer"
    elseif AimTargetMode == "Sheriff" then
        local role = RolesController.GetRole(player)
        return role == "Sheriff" or role == "Hero"
    elseif AimTargetMode == "Auto (by my tool)" then
        local character = Client.Character
        local backpack = Client:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        local hasGun = (character and character:FindFirstChild("Gun") ~= nil)
            or (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Gun") ~= nil)
        if hasGun then
            return RolesController.GetRole(player) == "Murderer"
        end
        return true
    end
    return true
end

-- Stops the "Show aim status" loop on unload.
local AimDebugToken = 0
Backshot.AimDebug = { Unload = function() AimDebugToken += 1 end }

-- "Stand can attack": the buddy hits whatever is in its reach, through the same kill
-- call Kill Aura and Silent Aim use (see backshot_silentaim.lua for how a hit is sent).
FunController.Config.AttackTargets = function()
    local list = {}
    local root = Client.Character and Client.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return list end
    for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
        if player ~= Client then
            local targetRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local humanoid = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if targetRoot and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
                table.insert(list, player)
            end
        end
    end
    return list
end
FunController.Config.OnAttackTick = function(player)
    SilentAimController.FireKill(player)
end

--
-- Player picker: a list with avatar, display name, username and role. Tap a row to choose.
local SelectedTarget = nil
local AvatarCache = {}

local function LoadAvatar(player, imageLabel)
    local cached = AvatarCache[player.UserId]
    if cached then
        imageLabel.Image = cached
        return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, content = pcall(function()
            return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(player.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100)
        end)
        if ok and content then
            AvatarCache[player.UserId] = content
            if imageLabel.Parent then imageLabel.Image = content end
        end
    end)
end

local PickerGui
local function ClosePicker()
    if PickerGui then
        PickerGui:Destroy()
        PickerGui = nil
    end
end

local function RolePriority(role)
    if role == "Murderer" then return 1 end
    if role == "Sheriff" or role == "Hero" then return 2 end
    return 3
end

local function OpenPicker(onClose)
    ClosePicker()
    local parent = GuiParent()
    if not parent then
        if onClose then onClose() end
        return
    end
    RolesController.Refresh()

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "BackshotPicker"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.DisplayOrder = 20002
    gui.Parent = parent
    PickerGui = gui

    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
    local width = math.min(340, viewport.X - 24)
    local height = math.min(380, viewport.Y - 24)

    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, height)
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 22, 30)
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Parent = gui
    local frameCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    frameCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    frameCorner.Parent = frame

    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Text = "Select player"
    title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    title.TextSize = 15
    title.Parent = frame

    local function finish(player)
        if player then
            SelectedTarget = player
            Notify("Target: " .. player.Name)
        end
        ClosePicker()
        if onClose then onClose() end
    end

    local function makeButton(text, index, count, action)
        local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
        local gap = 6
        local buttonWidth = (width - 16 - gap * (count - 1)) / count
        button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8 + (index - 1) * (buttonWidth + gap), 32)
        button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(buttonWidth, 28)
        button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(78, 139, 255)
        button.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        button.Text = text
        button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        button.TextSize = 13
        button.BorderSizePixel = 0
        button.Parent = frame
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        corner.Parent = button
        button.Activated:Connect(action)
    end

    makeButton("Murderer", 1, 3, function()
        local target = RolesController.FindByRole("Murderer")
        if target then finish(target) else Notify("No murderer detected yet") end
    end)
    makeButton("Sheriff", 2, 3, function()
        local target = RolesController.FindByRole("Sheriff")
        if target then finish(target) else Notify("No sheriff detected yet") end
    end)
    makeButton("Close", 3, 3, function() finish(nil) end)

    local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    list.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 68)
    list.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width - 16, height - 76)
    list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    list.BorderSizePixel = 0
    list.ScrollBarThickness = 4
    list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    list.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    list.Parent = frame
    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    layout.Parent = list

    local rows = {}

    local function buildRow(player)
        local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 52)
        row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 38, 50)
        row.Text = ""
        row.AutoButtonColor = true
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.Parent = list
        local rowCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        rowCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        rowCorner.Parent = row

        local avatar = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        avatar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6)
        avatar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 40)
        avatar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 27, 36)
        avatar.BorderSizePixel = 0
        avatar.Parent = row
        local avatarCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        avatarCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        avatarCorner.Parent = avatar
        LoadAvatar(player, avatar)

        local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nameLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(54, 7)
        nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -140, 0, 20)
        nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLabel.Text = player.DisplayName
        nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
        nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        nameLabel.TextSize = 14
        nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        nameLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        nameLabel.Parent = row

        local userLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        userLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(54, 27)
        userLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -140, 0, 18)
        userLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        userLabel.Text = "@" .. player.Name
        userLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 155, 170)
        userLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        userLabel.TextSize = 12
        userLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        userLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        userLabel.Parent = row

        local roleLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        roleLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
        roleLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0)
        roleLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(74, 20)
        roleLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        roleLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        roleLabel.TextSize = 12
        roleLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        roleLabel.Parent = row

        row.Activated:Connect(function() finish(player) end)
        return { row = row, role = roleLabel }
    end

    local function refresh()
        local seen = {}
        for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
            if player ~= Client then
                seen[player] = true
                local entry = rows[player]
                if not entry then
                    entry = buildRow(player)
                    rows[player] = entry
                end
                local role = RolesController.GetRole(player)
                entry.role.Text = role == "Unknown" and "" or role
                entry.role.TextColor3 = RolesController.GetColor(player)
                entry.row.LayoutOrder = RolePriority(role)
                entry.row.BackgroundColor3 = (SelectedTarget == player) and Color3.fromRGB(50, 80, 140) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 38, 50)
            end
        end
        for player, entry in pairs(rows) do
            if not seen[player] then
                entry.row:Destroy()
                rows[player] = nil
            end
        end
    end

    refresh()
    task.spawn(function()
        while PickerGui == gui do
            task.wait(1.5)
            if PickerGui == gui then refresh() end
        end
    end)
end

--
-- ESP colours and labels follow the detected role.
local RoleColorsEnabled, RoleTextEnabled = true, true
local function RoleColor(player)
    if RoleColorsEnabled then return RolesController.GetColor(player) end
    return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
end
ESPController.Config.Color = RoleColor
BoxController.Config.Color = RoleColor
ExtrasController.Config.HeadDotColor = RoleColor
ESPController.Config.ExtraText = function(player)
    if not RoleTextEnabled then return nil end
    local role = RolesController.GetRole(player)
    if role == "Unknown" then return nil end
    return "[" .. role .. "]"
end

--
-- Spectate the chosen player by pointing the camera at their humanoid.
local SpectateController = (function()
    local controller = {}
    local connection, savedSubject

    function controller.SetEnabled(state)
        if connection then
            connection:Disconnect()
            connection = nil
        end
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not state then
            if camera then
                local humanoid = Client.Character and Client.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid then
                    camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
                elseif savedSubject then
                    camera.CameraSubject = savedSubject
                end
            end
            return true
        end
        if not SelectedTarget or not camera then return false end
        savedSubject = camera.CameraSubject
        connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            local currentCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            local target = SelectedTarget
            local humanoid = target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if currentCamera and humanoid and currentCamera.CameraSubject ~= humanoid then
                currentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid
            end
        end)
        return true
    end

    function controller.Unload()
        controller.SetEnabled(false)
    end
    return controller
end)()
Backshot.Spectate = SpectateController

--
-- Menu
UI.ShowLoader = false
local Window = UI:AddWindow("Backshot", "", "Murder Mystery 2")
-- Hold-to-aim button (touch devices): press and hold to aim, release to stop. Drag to move it.
local function BuildHoldButton(label, x, y, onPress, onRelease)
    local parent = GuiParent()
    if not parent then return end

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "Backshot" .. label .. "Btn"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.DisplayOrder = 20003
    gui.Parent = parent

    local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    button.Position = UDim2.new(x, 0, y, 0)
    button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(54, 54)
    button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 22, 30)
    button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
    button.Text = label
    button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    button.TextSize = 13
    button.AutoButtonColor = false
    button.BorderSizePixel = 0
    button.Parent = gui

    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    corner.Parent = button
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Thickness = 2
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(78, 139, 255)
    stroke.Parent = button

    local dragStart, startCenter, moved, pressed = nil, nil, false, false

    local function isPointer(input)
        local kind = input.UserInputType
        return kind == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or kind == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
    end

    button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if not isPointer(input) then return end
        dragStart = input.Position
        startCenter = button.AbsolutePosition + button.AbsoluteSize / 2
        moved = false
    end)

    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragStart then return end
        local kind = input.UserInputType
        if kind ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and kind ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end

        local delta = Vector2.new(input.Position.X - dragStart.X, input.Position.Y - dragStart.Y)
        if not moved and delta.Magnitude >= 8 then
            moved = true
            if not pressed then
                pressed = true
                button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
                pcall(onPress)
            end
        end
        if moved then
            local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
            local x2 = math.clamp(startCenter.X + delta.X, 27, viewport.X - 27)
            local y2 = math.clamp(startCenter.Y + delta.Y, 27, viewport.Y - 27)
            button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x2, y2)
        end
    end)

    local function release()
        if pressed then
            pressed = false
            button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
            pcall(onRelease)
        end
        dragStart = nil
    end

    button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if not isPointer(input) then return end
        if not moved then
            pressed = true
            button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            pcall(onPress)
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if dragStart and isPointer(input) then release() end
    end)
end

BuildHoldButton("FLING", 0.88, 0.8, function()
    local target = SelectedTarget or RolesController.FindByRole("Murderer")
    if target then
        ToolsController.FlingPlayer(target, 2.2)
    else
        Notify("Touch fling: pick a player first")
    end
end, function() end)

local StandToggle
StandToggle = SecTarget:AddToggle("Stand on target", false, function(State)
    if State then
        if not SelectedTarget then
            Notify("Pick a player first")
            StandToggle:Set(false)
            return
        end
        if not StandController.Start(SelectedTarget.Name) then
            Notify("Stand: could not start")
            StandToggle:Set(false)
        end
    else
        StandController.Stop()
    end
end)
SecTarget:AddDropdown("Stand side", { "Left", "Right", "Behind" }, "Left", function(Value) StandController.Config.Side = Value end)
SecTarget:AddDropdown("Stand height", { "Level", "Above" }, "Level", function(Value) StandController.Config.Elevation = Value end)
SecTarget:AddSlider("Stand distance", 1, 10, 3, function(Value) StandController.Config.Distance = Value end)
SecTarget:AddDropdown("Stand pose", StandModule.PoseNames, "None", function(Value) StandController.SetPose(Value) end)
local CustomPoseToggle
CustomPoseToggle = SecTarget:AddToggle("Custom pose id (tap to enter)", false, function(State)
    if not State then
        StandController.Config.AnimationId = nil
        StandController.SetPose(StandController.Config.Pose)
        return
    end
    PromptText("Animation id for the pose (unverified)", "101562976762233", function(Text)
        local id = tonumber(Text)
        if not id then
            Notify("Custom pose: that is not a number")
            CustomPoseToggle:Set(false)
            return
        end
        StandController.Config.AnimationId = id
        StandController.Config.AnimationTime = 0
        StandController.SetPose(StandController.Config.Pose)
        Notify("Custom pose id set to " .. Text .. " (not verified, check in game)")
    end, function()
        CustomPoseToggle:Set(false)
    end)
end)
SecTarget:AddSlider("Nudge left/right", -10, 10, 0, function(Value) StandController.Config.NudgeX = Value end)
SecTarget:AddSlider("Nudge up/down", -10, 10, 0, function(Value) StandController.Config.NudgeY = Value end)

local SecActions = PlayersTab:AddSection("Actions on target", "right")
local TpToggle
TpToggle = SecActions:AddToggle("Teleport to target", false, function(State)
    if not State then return end
    local myRoot = Client.Character and Client.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local targetRoot = SelectedTarget and SelectedTarget.Character and SelectedTarget.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if myRoot and targetRoot then
        myRoot.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
    else
        Notify("Teleport: pick a player first")
    end
    TpToggle:Set(false)
end)
local SpectateToggle
SpectateToggle = SecActions:AddToggle("Spectate target", false, function(State)
    if not SpectateController.SetEnabled(State) and State then
        Notify("Spectate: pick a player first")
        SpectateToggle:Set(false)
    end
end)

local function OneShot(section, name, action)
    local toggle
    toggle = section:AddToggle(name, false, function(State)
        if not State then return end
        action()
        toggle:Set(false)
    end)
end

local function PickRole(role)
    local target = RolesController.FindByRole(role)
    if target then
        SelectedTarget = target
        Notify("Target: " .. target.Name)
    else
        Notify("No " .. string.lower(role) .. " detected yet")
    end
end

local function GoToRole(role)
    local target = RolesController.FindByRole(role)
    local myRoot = Client.Character and Client.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local targetRoot = target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if myRoot and targetRoot then
        myRoot.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
    else
        Notify("Teleport: no " .. string.lower(role) .. " detected yet")
    end
end

OneShot(SecActions, "Select murderer", function() PickRole("Murderer") end)
OneShot(SecActions, "Select sheriff", function() PickRole("Sheriff") end)
OneShot(SecActions, "Teleport to murderer", function() GoToRole("Murderer") end)
OneShot(SecActions, "Teleport to sheriff", function() GoToRole("Sheriff") end)

local FlingSeconds = 2.2
SecActions:AddSlider("Fling time (x0.1 s)", 10, 60, 22, function(Value) FlingSeconds = Value / 10 end)
OneShot(SecActions, "Fling target", function()
    if not SelectedTarget then
        Notify("Fling: pick a player first")
    else
        ToolsController.FlingPlayer(SelectedTarget, FlingSeconds)
    end
end)
OneShot(SecActions, "Fling murderer", function()
    local target = RolesController.FindByRole("Murderer")
    if target then ToolsController.FlingPlayer(target, FlingSeconds) else Notify("No murderer detected yet") end
end)
OneShot(SecActions, "Fling sheriff", function()
    local target = RolesController.FindByRole("Sheriff")
    if target then ToolsController.FlingPlayer(target, FlingSeconds) else Notify("No sheriff detected yet") end
end)
OneShot(SecActions, "Fling all", function()
    ToolsController.FlingPlayers(Players:GetPlayers(), FlingSeconds)
end)
OneShot(SecActions, "Cancel fling (if stuck)", function() ToolsController.CancelFling() end)

local SecFlingAura = PlayersTab:AddSection("Fling aura and anti fling", "left")
SecFlingAura:AddToggle("Fling aura", false, function(State) ToolsController.SetFlingAura(State) end)
SecFlingAura:AddSlider("Fling aura range", 4, 60, 15, function(Value) ToolsController.Config.FlingAuraRange = Value end)
SecFlingAura:AddSlider("Fling cooldown (s)", 1, 15, 6, function(Value) ToolsController.Config.FlingCooldown = Value end)
SecFlingAura:AddDropdown("Fling aura target", { "Everyone", "Murderer", "Sheriff" }, "Everyone", function(Value)
    ToolsController.Config.FlingFilter = function(player)
        if player == Client then return false end
        if Value == "Murderer" then return RolesController.GetRole(player) == "Murderer" end
        if Value == "Sheriff" then
            local role = RolesController.GetRole(player)
            return role == "Sheriff" or role == "Hero"
        end
        return true
    end
end)
