SecSheriffShoot:AddToggle("Silent gun (hooks your own click)", false, function(State) GunShotController.SetSilentGun(State) end)
OneShot(SecSheriffShoot, "Shoot murderer once", function() GunShotController.ShootOnce() end)
-- Off by default: the server decides the shot, so a wall in the way on your screen does
-- not stop it from registering, same idea as Candy's aim (it fires the remote, not a bullet).
SecSheriffShoot:AddToggle("Wall check (off = shoot through walls)", false, function(State) GunShotController.Config.CheckWalls = State end)
SecSheriffShoot:AddSlider("Gun cooldown (x0.1 s)", 5, 40, 15, function(Value) GunShotController.Config.Cooldown = Value / 10 end)
SecSheriffShoot:AddSlider("Gun prediction (x0.01 s)", 0, 30, 0, function(Value) GunShotController.Config.Prediction = Value / 100 end)

-- Skins tab
local SkinsTab = Window:AddTab("Skins", "")
local SecSkins = SkinsTab:AddSection("Skin changer", "left")
SkinsToggle = SecSkins:AddToggle("Skins on", false, function(State) SkinsController.SetEnabled(State) end)

local function skinNamesFor(kind)
    local names = { "None" }
    for _, entry in SkinsCatalog do
        if entry.kind == kind then table.insert(names, entry.name) end
    end
    return names
end

local SkinsToggle
local function applySkin(toolName, skinName)
    -- Picking a skin always turns the changer on; no separate step needed.
    SkinsController.SetEnabled(true)
    if SkinsToggle then SkinsToggle:Set(true, true) end

    if skinName == "None" then
        SkinsController.Config.Skins[toolName] = nil
        SkinsController.Reapply()
        Notify(toolName .. " skin reset")
        return
    end
    for _, entry in SkinsCatalog do
        if entry.name == skinName then
            local colour = entry.colour and Color3.fromRGB(entry.colour[1], entry.colour[2], entry.colour[3]) or nil
            SkinsController.Config.Skins[toolName] = {
                MeshId = entry.meshId and ("rbxassetid://" .. entry.meshId) or nil,
                TextureId = entry.textureId and ("rbxassetid://" .. entry.textureId) or nil,
                Color = colour,
            }
            SkinsController.Reapply()
            Notify(toolName .. " skin set to " .. skinName .. " -- only you see this")
            return
        end
    end
end

SecSkins:AddDropdown("Knife skin", skinNamesFor("Knife"), "None", function(Value) applySkin("Knife", Value) end)
SecSkins:AddDropdown("Gun skin", skinNamesFor("Gun"), "None", function(Value) applySkin("Gun", Value) end)
OneShot(SecSkins, "Reset all skins", function()
    table.clear(SkinsController.Config.Skins)
    SkinsController.SetEnabled(false)
    SkinsController.SetEnabled(true)
    Notify("Skins reset")
end)
local function customSkinToggle(toolName)
    local toggle
    toggle = SecSkins:AddToggle("Custom " .. string.lower(toolName) .. " mesh id (tap to enter)", false, function(State)
        if not State then
            SkinsController.Config.Skins[toolName] = nil
            SkinsController.Reapply()
            return
        end
        PromptText(toolName .. " mesh id (unverified)", "101562976762233", function(Text)
            local id = tonumber(Text)
            if not id then
                Notify("Custom skin: that is not a number")
                toggle:Set(false)
                return
            end
            SkinsController.SetEnabled(true)
            if SkinsToggle then SkinsToggle:Set(true, true) end
            SkinsController.Config.Skins[toolName] = { MeshId = "rbxassetid://" .. Text }
            SkinsController.Reapply()
            Notify(toolName .. " mesh set to " .. Text .. " -- not verified, only you would see it")
        end, function()
            toggle:Set(false)
        end)
    end)
end
customSkinToggle("Knife")
customSkinToggle("Gun")

-- Quick-pick ids you sent in chat: data only, not verified. Each button tries the id as a
-- mesh id on the chosen tool, so you do not have to retype them.
local QuickSkinIds = { "2330094560", "12381495147", "6675185436", "9552381221", "85358580639186" }
local QuickSkinChoice = QuickSkinIds[1]
SecSkins:AddDropdown("Quick id (from chat)", QuickSkinIds, QuickSkinIds[1], function(Value) QuickSkinChoice = Value end)
OneShot(SecSkins, "Apply quick id to Knife", function()
    SkinsController.SetEnabled(true)
    if SkinsToggle then SkinsToggle:Set(true, true) end
    SkinsController.Config.Skins["Knife"] = { MeshId = "rbxassetid://" .. QuickSkinChoice }
    SkinsController.Reapply()
    Notify("Knife mesh set to " .. QuickSkinChoice .. " (not verified)")
end)
OneShot(SecSkins, "Apply quick id to Gun", function()
    SkinsController.SetEnabled(true)
    if SkinsToggle then SkinsToggle:Set(true, true) end
    SkinsController.Config.Skins["Gun"] = { MeshId = "rbxassetid://" .. QuickSkinChoice }
    SkinsController.Reapply()
    Notify("Gun mesh set to " .. QuickSkinChoice .. " (not verified)")
end)

local SecSkinsNote = SkinsTab:AddSection("Note", "right")
-- Changing a skin only changes how the tool looks on your own screen; it does not touch
-- the game's drop key or your inventory, so dropping a tool works exactly as it always does.

-- Effects tab
local EffectsTab = Window:AddTab("Effects", "")
local SecEffects = EffectsTab:AddSection("Kill effects", "left")
SecEffects:AddToggle("Kill effects on", false, function(State) SilentAimController.SetKillEffects(State) end)
SecEffects:AddDropdown("Effect style", SilentAimModule.KillEffectStyles, "Explosion", function(Value)
    SilentAimController.Config.KillEffectStyle = Value
end)
local EffectColorPresets = { "Red", "Blue", "Green", "Purple", "Gold", "White" }
local EFFECT_COLORS = {
    Red = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60), Blue = Color3.fromRGB(60, 140, 255), Green = Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 120),
    Purple = Color3.fromRGB(180, 90, 255), Gold = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 60), White = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
}
SecEffects:AddDropdown("Effect colour", EffectColorPresets, "Red", function(Value)
    SilentAimController.Config.KillEffectColor = EFFECT_COLORS[Value]
end)

-- Misc tab
local MiscTab = Window:AddTab("Misc", "")

local SecFarm = MiscTab:AddSection("Auto farm", "left")
SecFarm:AddToggle("Auto farm coins", false, function(State) FarmController.SetEnabled(State) end)
SecFarm:AddToggle("Coin ESP", false, function(State) FarmController.SetCoinEsp(State) end)
SecFarm:AddToggle("Farm counter on screen", true, function(State) FarmController.Config.ShowHud = State end)
SecFarm:AddToggle("Stop when bag is full", true, function(State) FarmController.Config.StopWhenFull = State end)
SecFarm:AddSlider("Bag size", 10, 100, 50, function(Value) FarmController.Config.BagMax = Value end)
SecFarm:AddSlider("Interval (x0.1 s)", 1, 30, 6, function(Value) FarmController.Config.Interval = Value / 10 end)
SecFarm:AddSlider("Farm max distance", 50, 1000, 600, function(Value) FarmController.Config.MaxDistance = Value end)
OneShot(SecFarm, "Reset coin count", function() FarmController.ResetCount() end)
local FarmTargetToggle
FarmTargetToggle = SecFarm:AddToggle("Custom coin name (optional)", false, function(State)
    if not State then return end
    PromptText("Part name to farm (empty = default coin rules)", "", function(Text)
        if Text == "" then
            FarmController.Config.TargetFilter = nil
            Notify("Auto farm: using the default coin rules")
        else
            FarmController.Config.TargetFilter = function(part)
                return part.Name == Text
            end
            Notify("Auto farm: target is " .. Text)
        end
    end, function()
        FarmTargetToggle:Set(false)
    end)
end)

local SecBuild = MiscTab:AddSection("Build world", "right")
SecBuild:AddToggle("Build world", false, function(State)
    if State then
        if not BuilderController.Enter() then Notify("Build world: character not ready") end
    else
        BuilderController.Exit()
    end
end)
SecBuild:AddDropdown("Save slot", { "build1", "build2", "build3", "build4", "build5" }, "build1", function(Value)
    BuilderController.Config.SaveName = Value
end)
SecBuild:AddSlider("Grid (x0.5)", 1, 8, 2, function(Value) BuilderController.Config.Grid = Value / 2 end)
local PrefabChoice = "Tower"
SecBuild:AddDropdown("Prefab", BuilderModule.PrefabNames or { "Tower", "Bridge", "Room", "Stairs" }, "Tower", function(Value) PrefabChoice = Value end)
OneShot(SecBuild, "Stamp prefab", function() BuilderController.StampPrefab(PrefabChoice) end)

local SecScript = MiscTab:AddSection("Script", "left")
SecScript:AddToggle("Unload script", false, function(State)
    if State then UnloadAll() end
end)
